Modular **Component Architecture**  \n    11→2. Clean **RESTful MVC** separation  \n    12→3. Explicit **Service Layer** with well-defined contracts  \n    13→4. An in-process **Event Bus** to decouple real-time communication  \n    14→5. Zero-downtime **Plugin System** for user-contributed Tiles\n    15→\n    16→A high-level component diagram is shown below:\n    17→\n    18→```mermaid\n    19→graph TD\n    20→    subgraph Client\n    21→        A[Web UI] -->|WebSockets + REST| G\n    22→    end\n    23→\n    24→    subgraph Server\n    25→        G[API Gateway] --> B[Auth Service]\n    26→        G --> C[Dashboard Controller]\n    27→        C --> D[Event Bus]\n    28→        D --> E[Tile Runtime]\n    29→        E --> F[Plugin Loader]\n    30→        F --> K[/plugins/*]\n    31→        C --> H[Cache Layer]\n    32→        B --> I[DB]\n    33→        C --> I\n    34→    end\n    35→```\n    36→\n    37→---\n    38→\n    39→## 1. Directory Layout\n    40→\n    41→```text\n    42→MosaicBoardStudio/\n    43→├── apps/                     # Entry points (CLI, server, tools)\n    44→├── src/\n    45→│   ├── core/                 # EventBus, Tile runtime, PluginLoader\n    46→│   ├── web/                  # MVC controllers, HTTP routing\n    47→│   ├── services/             # Auth, Payment, Notification, etc.\n    48→│   ├── persistence/          # ORM repositories, migrations\n    49→│   └── util/                 # Logging, Error handling, Thread pools\n    50→├── plugins/                  # 3rd-party tiles (loaded at runtime)\n    51→├── assets/                   # Static files served by nginx\n    52→└── docs/                     # This file\n    53→```\n    54→\n    55→---\n    56→\n    57→## 2. Core Runtime Components\n    58→\n    59→### 2.1 Event Bus\n    60→\n    61→An ultra-light, lock-free message broker that allows Tiles, controllers, and\n    62→services to communicate without direct dependencies.  \n    63→The implementation is header-only, relies on `std::atomic` and a\n    64→publish-subscribe map guarded by a readers-writers spinlock.\n    65→\n    66→```cpp\n    67→// File: src/core/EventBus.hpp\n    68→#pragma once\n    69→#include <atomic>\n    70→#include <functional>\n    71→#include <memory>\n    72→#include <mutex>\n    73→#include <shared_mutex>\n    74→#include <string>\n    75→#include <typeindex>\n    76→#include <unordered_map>\n    77→#include <vector>\n    78→\n    79→namespace mosaic::core\n    80→{\n    81→    class Event\n    82→    {\n    83→    public:\n    84→        virtual ~Event() = default;\n    85→    };\n    86→\n    87→    using EventPtr = std::shared_ptr<Event>;\n    88→    using EventHandler = std::function<void(const EventPtr&)>;\n    89→\n    90→    class EventBus\n    91→    {\n    92→    public:\n    93→        template <typename EventT>\n    94→        void publish(const EventT& evt)\n    95→        {\n    96→            static_assert(std::is_base_of_v<Event, EventT>,\n    97→                          \"EventT must derive from Event\");\n    98→            auto instance = std::make_shared<EventT>(evt);\n    99→\n   100→            std::shared_lock lock(mutex_);\n   101→            auto it = subscribers_.find(typeid(EventT));\n   102→            if (it == subscribers_.end()) { return; }\n   103→\n   104→            /* Dispatch outside lock to avoid deadlocks */\n   105→            auto handlers = it->second;\n   106→            lock.unlock();\n   107→            for (auto& cb : handlers) { cb(instance); }\n   108→        }\n   109→\n   110→        template <typename EventT>\n   111→        void subscribe(EventHandler cb)\n   112→        {\n   113→            static_assert(std::is_base_of_v<Event, EventT>,\n   114→                          \"EventT must derive from Event\");\n   115→            std::unique_lock lock(mutex_);\n   116→            subscribers_[typeid(EventT)].push_back(std::move(cb));\n   117→        }\n   118→\n   119→    private:\n   120→        std::unordered_map<std::type_index, std::vector<EventHandler>> subscribers_;\n   121→        std::shared_mutex mutex_;\n   122→    };\n   123→} // namespace mosaic::core\n   124→```\n   125→\n   126→Error handling is intentionally *fail-fast*—clients guarantee their handlers\n   127→never throw. Any unhandled exception terminates the process, which is caught by\n   128→the supervisor (systemd / Kubernetes) and automatically restarts the pod.\n   129→\n   130→---\n   131→\n   132→### 2.2 Tile Contract\n   133→\n   134→Every Tile lives in its own shared library and derives from a common interface.\n   135→The runtime uses a thin `PluginLoader` (next section) to load the concrete\n   136→implementation at run-time.\n   137→\n   138→```cpp\n   139→// File: src/core/Tile.hpp\n   140→#pragma once\n   141→#include <nlohmann/json.hpp>\n   142→#include <string>\n   143→\n   144→namespace mosaic::core\n   145→{\n   146→    class Tile\n   147→    {\n   148→    public:\n   149→        virtual ~Tile() = default;\n   150→\n   151→        /** @brief Called once immediately after the Tile is instantiated */\n   152→        virtual void init(const nlohmann::json& config) = 0;\n   153→\n   154→        /** @brief Invoked whenever the Tile receives an event */\n   155→        virtual void onEvent(const EventPtr& evt) = 0;\n   156→\n   157→        /** @brief Render the current state into a JSON blob sent via WebSocket */\n   158→        virtual nlohmann::json render() const = 0;\n   159→\n   160→        /** @brief Optional: Periodic tick for animations (60 Hz by default) */\n   161→        virtual void update(double dt) { /* no-op by default */ }\n   162→    };\n   163→\n   164→    /* Factory signature used by the PluginLoader */\n   165→    using TileFactory = std::unique_ptr<Tile> (*)();\n   166→} // namespace mosaic::core\n   167→```\n   168→\n   169→---\n   170→\n   171→### 2.3 Plugin Loader\n   172→\n   173→Hot-swappable shared libraries are discovered in `/plugins` at startup or when\n   174→the `plugins.refresh` admin API is invoked.  \n   175→The loader is *POSIX-first* (`dlopen` / `dlsym`) with a thin abstraction for\n   176→other platforms.\n   177→\n   178→```cpp\n   179→// File: src/core/PluginLoader.cpp\n   180→#include \"PluginLoader.hpp\"\n   181→#include <dlfcn.h>\n   182→#include <filesystem>\n   183→#include <spdlog/spdlog.h>\n   184→\n   185→namespace mosaic::core\n   186→{\n   187→    namespace fs = std::filesystem;\n   188→\n   189→    struct SharedLibDeleter\n   190→    {\n   191→        void operator()(void* handle) const noexcept\n   192→        {\n   193→            if (handle) dlclose(handle);\n   194→        }\n   195→    };\n   196→\n   197→    std::vector<PluginLoader::Plugin> PluginLoader::discover(const fs::path& dir)\n   198→    {\n   199→        std::vector<Plugin> out;\n   200→        if (!fs::exists(dir)) { return out; }\n   201→\n   202→        for (auto& entry : fs::directory_iterator{dir})\n   203→        {\n   204→            if (entry.path().extension() != \".so\") { continue; }\n   205→\n   206→            auto handle = std::unique_ptr<void, SharedLibDeleter>{\n   207→                dlopen(entry.path().c_str(), RTLD_NOW | RTLD_LOCAL)};\n   208→\n   209→            if (!handle)\n   210→            {\n   211→                spdlog::error(\"Failed to load plugin {}: {}\", entry.path(),\n   212→                              dlerror());\n   213→                continue; // move on\n   214→            }\n   215→\n   216→            auto factory =\n   217→                reinterpret_cast<TileFactory>(dlsym(handle.get(), \"createTile\"));\n   218→\n   219→            if (!factory)\n   220→            {\n   221→                spdlog::warn(\n   222→                    \"Plugin {} does not expose expected symbol 'createTile'\",\n   223→                    entry.path());\n   224→                continue;\n   225→            }\n   226→\n   227→            out.push_back(Plugin{entry.path(), std::move(handle), factory});\n   228→            spdlog::info(\"Loaded plugin {}\", entry.path());\n   229→        }\n   230→        return out;\n   231→    }\n   232→} // namespace mosaic::core\n   233→```\n   234→\n   235→---\n   236→\n   237→## 3. RESTful MVC Layer\n   238→\n   239→The web subsystem uses **Crow** (a header-only micro-framework) as HTTP router.\n   240→Controllers are thin adaptors that convert HTTP calls into service invocations\n   241→or EventBus messages.\n   242→\n   243→```cpp\n   244→// File: src/web/DashboardController.cpp\n   245→#include \"core/EventBus.hpp\"\n   246→#include \"services/CacheService.hpp\"\n   247→#include <crow.h>\n   248→#include <nlohmann/json.hpp>\n   249→\n   250→using namespace mosaic;\n   251→\n   252→void registerDashboardRoutes(crow::SimpleApp& app,\n   253→                             core::EventBus& bus,\n   254→                             services::CacheService& cache)\n   255→{\n   256→    //  GET /api/v1/dashboard/:id\n   257→    CROW_ROUTE(app, \"/api/v1/dashboard/<string>\")\n   258→        .methods(\"GET\"_method)([&](const std::string& id) {\n   259→            if (auto cached = cache.get(id); cached)\n   260→            {\n   261→                return crow::response{cached.value()};\n   262→            }\n   263→\n   264→            // fetch from DB (Repository pattern)\n   265→            auto repo = persistence::DashboardRepository{};\n   266→            auto dto  = repo.getById(id);\n   267→            if (!dto)\n   268→            {\n   269→                return crow::response(404, \"dashboard not found\");\n   270→            }\n   271→\n   272→            auto json = dto->toJson();\n   273→            cache.put(id, json.dump(), std::chrono::minutes{10});\n   274→            return crow::response{json.dump()};\n   275→        });\n   276→\n   277→    //  POST /api/v1/dashboard/:id/events\n   278→    CROW_ROUTE(app, \"/api/v1/dashboard/<string>/events\")\n   279→        .methods(\"POST\"_method)([&](const crow::request& req,\n   280→                                    const std::string& id) {\n   281→            auto body = nlohmann::json::parse(req.body);\n   282→            auto evt  = std::make_shared<core::Event>(); // simplified\n   283→            bus.publish(*evt); // fan-out to tiles\n   284→            return crow::response{202};\n   285→        });\n   286→}\n   287→```\n   288→\n   289→---\n   290→\n   291→## 4. Service Layer\n   292→\n   293→Services encapsulate business logic **(Authentication, Payment, Search, etc.)**.\n   294→They receive **DTOs** from controllers, interact with repositories and third-party SDKs, and\n   295→return sanitized DTOs to the caller.  \n   296→Each service is an interface with one production implementation and a second\n   297→*in-memory* stub used by unit tests.\n   298→\n   299→```cpp\n   300→// File: src/services/AuthService.hpp\n   301→#pragma once\n   302→#include <optional>\n   303→#include <string>\n   304→\n   305→namespace mosaic::services\n   306→{\n   307→    struct UserDTO\n   308→    {\n   309→        std::string id;\n   310→        std::string email;\n   311→        std::string displayName;\n   312→        bool        emailVerified{};\n   313→    };\n   314→\n   315→    class AuthService\n   316→    {\n   317→    public:\n   318→        virtual ~AuthService() = default;\n   319→\n   320→        virtual std::optional<UserDTO> 338→namespace mosaic::web\n   339→{\n   340→    struct AuthCtx\n   341→    {\n   342→        std::optional<services::UserDTO> user;\n   343→    };\n   344→\n   345→    struct AuthMiddleware\n   346→    {\n   347→        struct context\n   348→        {\n   349→            AuthCtx data;\n   350→        };\n   351→\n   352→        AuthMiddleware(services::AuthService& svc) : svc_{svc} {}\n   353→\n   354→        void before_handle(crow::request& req, crow::response&,\n   355→                           context& ctx)\n   356→        {\n   357→            auto auth = };\n   372→} // namespace mosaic::web\n   373→```\n   374→\n   375→---\n   376→\n   377→## 5. Persistence Layer (Repository + ORM)\n   378→\n   379→The platform ships with a light wrapper around **SQLite** for rapid prototyping\n   380→and can switch to **PostgreSQL** in production without touching business logic.\n   381→\n   382→```cpp\n   383→// File: src/persistence/DashboardRepository.cpp\n   384→#include \"DashboardRepository.hpp\"\n   385→#include \"util/Sqlite.hpp\"\n   386→#include <spdlog/spdlog.h>\n   387→\n   388→using namespace mosaic::persistence;\n   389→\n   390→std::optional<DashboardDTO> DashboardRepository::getById(\n   391→    const std::string& id) const\n   392→{\n   393→    static constexpr auto sql =\n   394→        \"SELECT id, owner_id, title, layout_json \"\n   395→        \"FROM dashboards WHERE id = ?1 LIMIT 1\";\n   396→\n   397→    auto stmt = util::Sqlite::prepare(sql);\n   398→    sqlite3_bind_text(stmt.get(), 1, id.c_str(), -1, SQLITE_TRANSIENT);\n   399→\n   400→    if (sqlite3_step(stmt.get()) == SQLITE_ROW)\n   401→    {\n   402→        DashboardDTO dto;\n   403→        dto.id         = reinterpret_cast<const char*>(sqlite3_column_text(stmt.get(), 0));\n   404→        dto.ownerId    = reinterpret_cast<const char*>(sqlite3_column_text(stmt.get(), 1));\n   405→        dto.title      = reinterpret_cast<const char*>(sqlite3_column_text(stmt.get(), 2));\n   406→\n   407→        const char* layout = reinterpret_cast<const char*>(sqlite3_column_text(stmt.get(), 3));\n   408→        dto.layout         = nlohmann::json::parse(layout);\n   409→\n   410→        return dto;\n   411→    }\n   412→    spdlog::info(\"Dashboard {} not found\", id);\n   413→    return std::nullopt;\n   414→}\n   415→```\n   416→\n   417→---\n   418→\n   419→## 6. Cache Layer\n   420→\n   421→A *2-tier* strategy:\n   422→\n   423→1. **In-process**: a sharded `unordered_map` with TTL (μ-seconds resolution).  \n   424→2. **Redis**: optional, distributed, used only when multiple instances run.\n   425→\n   426→```cpp\n   427→// File: src/services/CacheService.hpp (excerpt)\n   428→bool CacheService::put(const std::string& key,\n   429→                       const std::string& value,\n   430→                       std::chrono::seconds ttl)\n   431→{\n   432→    auto expires = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + ttl;\n   433→    std::unique_lock lock(shards_[hash(key) % shards_.size()].mutex);\n   434→    shards_[hash(key) % shards_.size()].map[key] = {value, expires};\n   435→    return true;\n   436→}\n   437→```\n   438→\n   439→---\n   440→\n   441→## 7. Threading & Concurrency\n   442→\n   443→*   **Crow** uses one thread per CPU core.  \n   444→*   **EventBus** is lock-free for publish path; subscription path is guarded.  \n   445→*   **Tile::update** is executed on a dedicated **60 Hz** worker pool.  \n   446→*   All async IO (DB, Redis, Stripe SDK) is performed with\n   447→    `boost::asio::io_context`.\n   448→\n   449→---\n   450→\n   451→## 8. Error Handling & Logging\n   452→\n   453→*   All public functions return `std::expected<T, Error>` or `std::optional<T>`\n   454→    where appropriate.  \n   455→*   Fatal errors are logged with `spdlog::critical` and escalate via exceptions.  \n   456→*   The root `main()` sets `std::set_terminate` to log the stacktrace with\n   457→    **backward-cpp** before aborting.\n   458→\n   459→---\n   460→\n   461→## 9. Build & Deployment\n   462→\n   463→1. **CMake 3.26** generates *compile_commands.json* for clang-tidy & clang-format.  \n   464→2. **Conan** handles third-party dependencies.  \n   465→3. **GitHub Actions** runs unit tests, static analysis, and publishes a Docker\n   466→   image tagged `ghcr.io/mosaicboard/studio:<commit>`.  \n   467→4. Production is deployed to **Kubernetes**; each pod exposes `8080/TCP` and\n   468→   connects to a shared Redis & PostgreSQL cluster via **Istio** mTLS.\n   469→\n   470→---\n   471→\n   472→## 10. Extending MosaicBoard Studio\n   473→\n   474→1. Clone the starter template in `examples/tiles/HelloWave`.  \n   475→2. Implement the `Tile` interface.  \n   476→3. Export a factory symbol:\n   477→\n   478→   ```cpp\n   479→   extern \"C\" ::mosaic::core::Tile* createTile()\n   480→   {\n   481→       return new HelloWaveTile{};\n   482→   }\n   483→   ```\n   484→\n   485→4. Build: `cmake --preset release && make`  \n   486→5. Drop the resulting `libHelloWave.so` into the `plugins/` directory.  \n   487→6. The dashboard automatically detects and mounts the new Tile—**hot reload,\n   488→   no restart required!**\n   489→\n   490→---\n   491→\n   492→### Appendix A – Sequence: WebSocket Event → Tile Render\n   493→\n   494→```mermaid\n   495→sequenceDiagram\n   496→    participant Client\n   497→    participant Gateway\n   498→    participant EventBus\n   499→    participant Tile\n   500→    Client->>Gateway: WebSocket {\"click\":\"button1\"}\n   501→    Gateway->>EventBus: publish(InputEvent)\n   502→    EventBus-->>Tile: InputEvent\n   503→    Tile->>Tile: update()\n   504→    Tile-->>Gateway: render() JSON\n   505→    Gateway-->>Client: {\"type\":\"patch\", ...}\n   506→```\n   507→\n   508→---\n   509→\n   510→© 2024 MosaicBoard Studio. Modular **Component Architecture**  \n2. Clean **RESTful MVC** separation  \n3. Explicit **Service Layer** with well-defined contracts  \n4. An in-process **Event Bus** to decouple real-time communication  \n5. Zero-downtime **Plugin System** for user-contributed Tiles\n\nA high-level component diagram is shown below:\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TD\n    subgraph Client\n        A[Web UI] -->|WebSockets + REST| G\n    end\n\n    subgraph Server\n        G[API Gateway] --> B[Auth Service]\n        G --> C[Dashboard Controller]\n        C --> D[Event Bus]\n        D --> E[Tile Runtime]\n        E --> F[Plugin Loader]\n        F --> K[/plugins/*]\n        C --> H[Cache Layer]\n        B --> I[DB]\n        C --> I\n    end\n```\n\n---\n\n## 1. Directory Layout\n\n```text\nMosaicBoardStudio/\n├── apps/                     # Entry points (CLI, server, tools)\n├── src/\n│   ├── core/                 # EventBus, Tile runtime, PluginLoader\n│   ├── web/                  # MVC controllers, HTTP routing\n│   ├── services/             # Auth, Payment, Notification, etc.\n│   ├── persistence/          # ORM repositories, migrations\n│   └── util/                 # Logging, Error handling, Thread pools\n├── plugins/                  # 3rd-party tiles (loaded at runtime)\n├── assets/                   # Static files served by nginx\n└── docs/                     # This file\n```\n\n---\n\n## 2. Core Runtime Components\n\n### 2.1 Event Bus\n\nAn ultra-light, lock-free message broker that allows Tiles, controllers, and\nservices to communicate without direct dependencies.  \nThe implementation is header-only, relies on `std::atomic` and a\npublish-subscribe map guarded by a readers-writers spinlock.\n\n```cpp\n// File: src/core/EventBus.hpp\n#pragma once\n#include <atomic>\n#include <functional>\n#include <memory>\n#include <mutex>\n#include <shared_mutex>\n#include <string>\n#include <typeindex>\n#include <unordered_map>\n#include <vector>\n\nnamespace mosaic::core\n{\n    class Event\n    {\n    public:\n        virtual ~Event() = default;\n    };\n\n    using EventPtr = std::shared_ptr<Event>;\n    using EventHandler = std::function<void(const EventPtr&)>;\n\n    class EventBus\n    {\n    public:\n        template <typename EventT>\n        void publish(const EventT& evt)\n        {\n            static_assert(std::is_base_of_v<Event, EventT>,\n                          \"EventT must derive from Event\");\n            auto instance = std::make_shared<EventT>(evt);\n\n            std::shared_lock lock(mutex_);\n            auto it = subscribers_.find(typeid(EventT));\n            if (it == subscribers_.end()) { return; }\n\n            /* Dispatch outside lock to avoid deadlocks */\n            auto handlers = it->second;\n            lock.unlock();\n            for (auto& cb : handlers) { cb(instance); }\n        }\n\n        template <typename EventT>\n        void subscribe(EventHandler cb)\n        {\n            static_assert(std::is_base_of_v<Event, EventT>,\n                          \"EventT must derive from Event\");\n            std::unique_lock lock(mutex_);\n            subscribers_[typeid(EventT)].push_back(std::move(cb));\n        }\n\n    private:\n        std::unordered_map<std::type_index, std::vector<EventHandler>> subscribers_;\n        std::shared_mutex mutex_;\n    };\n} // namespace mosaic::core\n```\n\nError handling is intentionally *fail-fast*—clients guarantee their handlers\nnever throw. Any unhandled exception terminates the process, which is caught by\nthe supervisor (systemd / Kubernetes) and automatically restarts the pod.\n\n---\n\n### 2.2 Tile Contract\n\nEvery Tile lives in its own shared library and derives from a common interface.\nThe runtime uses a thin `PluginLoader` (next section) to load the concrete\nimplementation at run-time.\n\n```cpp\n// File: src/core/Tile.hpp\n#pragma once\n#include <nlohmann/json.hpp>\n#include <string>\n\nnamespace mosaic::core\n{\n    class Tile\n    {\n    public:\n        virtual ~Tile() = default;\n\n        /** @brief Called once immediately after the Tile is instantiated */\n        virtual void init(const nlohmann::json& config) = 0;\n\n        /** @brief Invoked whenever the Tile receives an event */\n        virtual void onEvent(const EventPtr& evt) = 0;\n\n        /** @brief Render the current state into a JSON blob sent via WebSocket */\n        virtual nlohmann::json render() const = 0;\n\n        /** @brief Optional: Periodic tick for animations (60 Hz by default) */\n        virtual void update(double dt) { /* no-op by default */ }\n    };\n\n    /* Factory signature used by the PluginLoader */\n    using TileFactory = std::unique_ptr<Tile> (*)();\n} // namespace mosaic::core\n```\n\n---\n\n### 2.3 Plugin Loader\n\nHot-swappable shared libraries are discovered in `/plugins` at startup or when\nthe `plugins.refresh` admin API is invoked.  \nThe loader is *POSIX-first* (`dlopen` / `dlsym`) with a thin abstraction for\nother platforms.\n\n```cpp\n// File: src/core/PluginLoader.cpp\n#include \"PluginLoader.hpp\"\n#include <dlfcn.h>\n#include <filesystem>\n#include <spdlog/spdlog.h>\n\nnamespace mosaic::core\n{\n    namespace fs = std::filesystem;\n\n    struct SharedLibDeleter\n    {\n        void operator()(void* handle) const noexcept\n        {\n            if (handle) dlclose(handle);\n        }\n    };\n\n    std::vector<PluginLoader::Plugin> PluginLoader::discover(const fs::path& dir)\n    {\n        std::vector<Plugin> out;\n        if (!fs::exists(dir)) { return out; }\n\n        for (auto& entry : fs::directory_iterator{dir})\n        {\n            if (entry.path().extension() != \".so\") { continue; }\n\n            auto handle = std::unique_ptr<void, SharedLibDeleter>{\n                dlopen(entry.path().c_str(), RTLD_NOW | RTLD_LOCAL)};\n\n            if (!handle)\n            {\n                spdlog::error(\"Failed to load plugin {}: {}\", entry.path(),\n                              dlerror());\n                continue; // move on\n            }\n\n            auto factory =\n                reinterpret_cast<TileFactory>(dlsym(handle.get(), \"createTile\"));\n\n            if (!factory)\n            {\n                spdlog::warn(\n                    \"Plugin {} does not expose expected symbol 'createTile'\",\n                    entry.path());\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            out.push_back(Plugin{entry.path(), std::move(handle), factory});\n            spdlog::info(\"Loaded plugin {}\", entry.path());\n        }\n        return out;\n    }\n} // namespace mosaic::core\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. RESTful MVC Layer\n\nThe web subsystem uses **Crow** (a header-only micro-framework) as HTTP router.\nControllers are thin adaptors that convert HTTP calls into service invocations\nor EventBus messages.\n\n```cpp\n// File: src/web/DashboardController.cpp\n#include \"core/EventBus.hpp\"\n#include \"services/CacheService.hpp\"\n#include <crow.h>\n#include <nlohmann/json.hpp>\n\nusing namespace mosaic;\n\nvoid registerDashboardRoutes(crow::SimpleApp& app,\n                             core::EventBus& bus,\n                             services::CacheService& cache)\n{\n    //  GET /api/v1/dashboard/:id\n    CROW_ROUTE(app, \"/api/v1/dashboard/<string>\")\n        .methods(\"GET\"_method)([&](const std::string& id) {\n            if (auto cached = cache.get(id); cached)\n            {\n                return crow::response{cached.value()};\n            }\n\n            // fetch from DB (Repository pattern)\n            auto repo = persistence::DashboardRepository{};\n            auto dto  = repo.getById(id);\n            if (!dto)\n            {\n                return crow::response(404, \"dashboard not found\");\n            }\n\n            auto json = dto->toJson();\n            cache.put(id, json.dump(), std::chrono::minutes{10});\n            return crow::response{json.dump()};\n        });\n\n    //  POST /api/v1/dashboard/:id/events\n    CROW_ROUTE(app, \"/api/v1/dashboard/<string>/events\")\n        .methods(\"POST\"_method)([&](const crow::request& req,\n                                    const std::string& id) {\n            auto body = nlohmann::json::parse(req.body);\n            auto evt  = std::make_shared<core::Event>(); // simplified\n            bus.publish(*evt); // fan-out to tiles\n            return crow::response{202};\n        });\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. Service Layer\n\nServices encapsulate business logic **(Authentication, Payment, Search, etc.)**.\nThey receive **DTOs** from controllers, interact with repositories and third-party SDKs, and\nreturn sanitized DTOs to the caller.  \nEach service is an interface with one production implementation and a second\n*in-memory* stub used by unit tests.\n\n```cpp\n// File: src/services/AuthService.hpp\n#pragma once\n#include <optional>\n#include <string>\n\nnamespace mosaic::services\n{\n    struct UserDTO\n    {\n        std::string id;\n        std::string email;\n        std::string displayName;\n        bool        emailVerified{};\n    };\n\n    class AuthService\n    {\n    public:\n        virtual ~AuthService() = default;\n\n        virtual std::optional<UserDTO> verifyBearerToken(\n            const std::string& jwt) = 0;\n\n        virtual std::string createCustomToken(const std::string& uid,\n                                              std::int64_t      expiresInSec) = 0;\n    };\n} // namespace mosaic::services\n```\n\n#### Authentication middleware (Crow):\n\n```cpp\n// File: src/web/middleware/AuthMiddleware.hpp\n#pragma once\n#include \"services/AuthService.hpp\"\n#include <crow.h>\n#include <nlohmann/json.hpp>\n\nnamespace mosaic::web\n{\n    struct AuthCtx\n    {\n        std::optional<services::UserDTO> user;\n    };\n\n    struct AuthMiddleware\n    {\n        struct context\n        {\n            AuthCtx data;\n        };\n\n        AuthMiddleware(services::AuthService& svc) : svc_{svc} {}\n\n        void before_handle(crow::request& req, crow::response&,\n                           context& ctx)\n        {\n            auto auth = req.get_header_value(\"Authorization\");\n            if (auth.rfind(\"Bearer \", 0) != 0) { return; } // anonymous\n            auto jwt = auth.substr(7);\n            ctx.data.user = svc_.verifyBearerToken(jwt);\n            if (!ctx.data.user)\n            {\n                throw crow::error{401, \"Invalid or expired token\"};\n            }\n        }\n\n        void after_handle(crow::request&, crow::response&, context&) {}\n\n    private:\n        services::AuthService& svc_;\n    };\n} // namespace mosaic::web\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Persistence Layer (Repository + ORM)\n\nThe platform ships with a light wrapper around **SQLite** for rapid prototyping\nand can switch to **PostgreSQL** in production without touching business logic.\n\n```cpp\n// File: src/persistence/DashboardRepository.cpp\n#include \"DashboardRepository.hpp\"\n#include \"util/Sqlite.hpp\"\n#include <spdlog/spdlog.h>\n\nusing namespace mosaic::persistence;\n\nstd::optional<DashboardDTO> DashboardRepository::getById(\n    const std::string& id) const\n{\n    static constexpr auto sql =\n        \"SELECT id, owner_id, title, layout_json \"\n        \"FROM dashboards WHERE id = ?1 LIMIT 1\";\n\n    auto stmt = util::Sqlite::prepare(sql);\n    sqlite3_bind_text(stmt.get(), 1, id.c_str(), -1, SQLITE_TRANSIENT);\n\n    if (sqlite3_step(stmt.get()) == SQLITE_ROW)\n    {\n        DashboardDTO dto;\n        dto.id         = reinterpret_cast<const char*>(sqlite3_column_text(stmt.get(), 0));\n        dto.ownerId    = reinterpret_cast<const char*>(sqlite3_column_text(stmt.get(), 1));\n        dto.title      = reinterpret_cast<const char*>(sqlite3_column_text(stmt.get(), 2));\n\n        const char* layout = reinterpret_cast<const char*>(sqlite3_column_text(stmt.get(), 3));\n        dto.layout         = nlohmann::json::parse(layout);\n\n        return dto;\n    }\n    spdlog::info(\"Dashboard {} not found\", id);\n    return std::nullopt;\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 6. Cache Layer\n\nA *2-tier* strategy:\n\n1. **In-process**: a sharded `unordered_map` with TTL (μ-seconds resolution).  \n2. **Redis**: optional, distributed, used only when multiple instances run.\n\n```cpp\n// File: src/services/CacheService.hpp (excerpt)\nbool CacheService::put(const std::string& key,\n                       const std::string& value,\n                       std::chrono::seconds ttl)\n{\n    auto expires = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + ttl;\n    std::unique_lock lock(shards_[hash(key) % shards_.size()].mutex);\n    shards_[hash(key) % shards_.size()].map[key] = {value, expires};\n    return true;\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 7. Threading & Concurrency\n\n*   **Crow** uses one thread per CPU core.  \n*   **EventBus** is lock-free for publish path; subscription path is guarded.  \n*   **Tile::update** is executed on a dedicated **60 Hz** worker pool.  \n*   All async IO (DB, Redis, Stripe SDK) is performed with\n    `boost::asio::io_context`.\n\n---\n\n## 8. Error Handling & Logging\n\n*   All public functions return `std::expected<T, Error>` or `std::optional<T>`\n    where appropriate.  \n*   Fatal errors are logged with `spdlog::critical` and escalate via exceptions.  \n*   The root `main()` sets `std::set_terminate` to log the stacktrace with\n    **backward-cpp** before aborting.\n\n---\n\n## 9. Build & Deployment\n\n1. **CMake 3.26** generates *compile_commands.json* for clang-tidy & clang-format.  \n2. **Conan** handles third-party dependencies.  \n3. **GitHub Actions** runs unit tests, static analysis, and publishes a Docker\n   image tagged `ghcr.io/mosaicboard/studio:<commit>`.  \n4. Production is deployed to **Kubernetes**; each pod exposes `8080/TCP` and\n   connects to a shared Redis & PostgreSQL cluster via **Istio** mTLS.\n\n---\n\n## 10. Extending MosaicBoard Studio\n\n1. Clone the starter template in `examples/tiles/HelloWave`.  \n2. Implement the `Tile` interface.  \n3. Export a factory symbol:\n\n   ```cpp\n   extern \"C\" ::mosaic::core::Tile* createTile()\n   {\n       return new HelloWaveTile{};\n   }\n   ```\n\n4. Build: `cmake --preset release && make`  \n5. Drop the resulting `libHelloWave.so` into the `plugins/` directory.  \n6. The dashboard automatically detects and mounts the new Tile—**hot reload,\n   no restart required!**\n\n---\n\n### Appendix A – Sequence: WebSocket Event → Tile Render\n\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    participant Client\n    participant Gateway\n    participant EventBus\n    participant Tile\n    Client->>Gateway: WebSocket {\"click\":\"button1\"}\n    Gateway->>EventBus: publish(InputEvent)\n    EventBus-->>Tile: InputEvent\n    Tile->>Tile: update()\n    Tile-->>Gateway: render() JSON\n    Gateway-->>Client: {\"type\":\"patch\", ...}\n```\n\n---\n\n© 2024 MosaicBoard Studio. All rights reserved.\n```","numLines":511,"startLine":1,"totalLines":511}}}

Host performs a **signature & version check**.  \n    56→2. `CreatePlugin` is called once; the returned object persists for the dashboard session.  \n    57→3. On hot-reload or shutdown, `DestroyPlugin` is invoked.  \n    58→4. If a compilation error occurs, the host falls back to the previous working binary (atomic swap).  \n    59→\n    60→---\n    61→\n    62→## 3  Creating Your First Tile\n    63→\n    64→Below is a **minimal but production-grade** example that renders a real-time UTC clock, exposes a REST endpoint, and demonstrates event-bus publishing.  Use it as a reference for your own tiles.\n    65→\n    66→### 3.1  Directory Layout\n    67→```\n    68→ClockTile/\n    69→├─ include/\n    70→│  └─ ClockTile.hpp\n    71→├─ src/\n    72→│  ├─ ClockTile.cpp\n    73→│  └─ PluginMain.cpp\n    74→├─ tests/\n    75→│  └─ test_ClockTile.cpp\n    76→├─ CMakeLists.txt\n    77→└─ tile.manifest.json\n    78→```\n    79→\n    80→### 3.2  Header (`include/ClockTile.hpp`)\n    81→```cpp\n    82→#pragma once\n    83→/****************************************************************************************\n    84→ * ClockTile — A reference implementation of an always-on UTC clock.\n    85→ *\n    86→ * © 2024 Your Company.  Licensed under the MIT License.\n    87→ ****************************************************************************************/\n    88→#include <mosaic/api/ITile.hpp>          // Core rendering surface\n    89→#include <mosaic/event/IEventBus.hpp>    // Publish/subscribe\n    90→#include <chrono>\n    91→#include <mutex>\n    92→\n    93→class ClockTile final : public mosaic::api::ITile\n    94→{\n    95→public:\n    96→    explicit ClockTile(const mosaic::api::TileContext& ctx);\n    97→    ~ClockTile() noexcept override = default;\n    98→\n    99→    // ITile overrides\n   100→    void onAttach(mosaic::api::Surface& surface) override;\n   101→    void onDetach() noexcept override;\n   102→    void onTick(std::chrono::milliseconds delta) override;\n   103→    void onHttpRequest(const mosaic::net::HttpRequest& req,\n   104→                       mosaic::net::HttpResponse& res) override;\n   105→\n   106→private:\n   107→    void updateModel();\n   108→    void publishTimeChanged() const;\n   109→\n   110→    std::chrono::system_clock::time_point _now;\n   111→    std::mutex                             _mutex;\n   112→    mosaic::api::Surface*                  _surface {nullptr};\n   113→    mosaic::event::EventBus&               _bus;\n   114→};\n   115→```\n   116→\n   117→### 3.3  Implementation (`src/ClockTile.cpp`)\n   118→```cpp\n   119→#include \"ClockTile.hpp\"\n   120→#include <mosaic/log/Log.hpp>\n   121→#include <fmt/format.h>\n   122→\n   123→using namespace std::chrono_literals;\n   124→\n   125→ClockTile::ClockTile(const mosaic::api::TileContext& ctx)\n   126→    : ITile(ctx)\n   127→    , _bus(ctx.eventBus)\n   128→{\n   129→    updateModel();\n   130→}\n   131→\n   132→void ClockTile::onAttach(mosaic::api::Surface& surface)\n   133→{\n   134→    _surface = &surface;\n   135→    surface.setMinRefreshInterval(1s);      // 1Hz refresh\n   136→    mosaic::log::info(\"ClockTile attached.\");\n   137→}\n   138→\n   139→void ClockTile::onDetach() noexcept\n   140→{\n   141→    mosaic::log::info(\"ClockTile detached.\");\n   142→    _surface = nullptr;\n   143→}\n   144→\n   145→void ClockTile::onTick(std::chrono::milliseconds /*delta*/)\n   146→{\n   147→    // Update internal state once per second.\n   148→    std::lock_guard lk(_mutex);\n   149→    auto newNow = std::chrono::system_clock::now();\n   150→    if (std::chrono::floor<std::chrono::seconds>(newNow) !=\n   151→        std::chrono::floor<std::chrono::seconds>(_now))\n   152→    {\n   153→        _now = newNow;\n   154→        updateModel();\n   155→        publishTimeChanged();\n   156→    }\n   157→}\n   158→\n   159→void ClockTile::onHttpRequest(const mosaic::net::HttpRequest& req,\n   160→                              mosaic::net::HttpResponse& res)\n   161→{\n   162→    if (req.method() == \"GET\" && req.target() == \"/now\")\n   163→    {\n   164→        std::lock_guard lk(_mutex);\n   165→        const auto iso = fmt::format(\"{:%FT%TZ}\", std::chrono::floor<std::chrono::seconds>(_now));\n   166→        res.status(200)\n   167→           .contentType(\"application/json\")\n   168→           .body(fmt::format(R\"({{\"utc\":\"{}\"}})\", iso));\n   169→    }\n   170→    else\n   171→    {\n   172→        res.status(404)\n   173→           .body(R\"({\"error\":\"endpoint_not_found\"})\");\n   174→    }\n   175→}\n   176→\n   177→void ClockTile::updateModel()\n   178→{\n   179→    if (!_surface) return;\n   180→\n   181→    const auto iso = fmt::format(\"{:%H:%M:%S}\", std::chrono::floor<std::chrono::seconds>(_now));\n   182→    _surface->setProperty(\"text\", iso);\n   183→}\n   184→\n   185→void ClockTile::publishTimeChanged() const\n   186→{\n   187→    _bus.publish(\"clock/updated\", [this](mosaic::event::Message& msg){\n   188→        std::lock_guard lk(_mutex);\n   189→        msg.set(\"timestamp\", std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(\n   190→                                 _now.time_since_epoch()).count());\n   191→    });\n   192→}\n   193→```\n   194→\n   195→### 3.4  Plugin Entry Point (`src/PluginMain.cpp`)\n   196→```cpp\n   197→#define MOSAIC_PLUGIN_EXPORTS\n   198→#include <mosaic/plugin/PluginAPI.hpp>\n   199→#include \"ClockTile.hpp\"\n   200→\n   201→using  290→\n   291→TEST(ClockTile, HttpNowReturnsIso8601)\n   292→{\n   293→    mosaic::testing::TileTestHarness harness;\n   294→    auto tile = harness.create<ClockTile>();\n   295→\n   296→ Advanced Topics\n   309→\n   310→### 4.1  Asynchronous Data Streams\n   311→Your tile can spawn background tasks via `ctx.executor`, which is a thread-pool configured for *short-lived, non-blocking* jobs. Remember to call `_surface->requestRepaint()` from the main thread only.\n   312→\n   313→### 4.2  The Real-Time Event Bus\n   314→Use topic naming conventions: `namespace/subsystem/action`.  \n   315→Events can be **sticky** (retained) or **volatile** (fire-and-forget). Declare them in your manifest for subscriber auto-completion inside the Visual Composer.\n   316→\n   317→### 4.3  Secure Settings & Secrets\n   318→Sensitive data (API keys, DB credentials) should **never** be hard-coded.  \n   319→Use the Settings service:\n   320→\n   321→```cpp\n   322→auto secret = ctx.settings().getSecret(\"my_service.api_key\");\n   323→```\n   324→\n   325→---\n   326→\n   327→## 5  Packaging & Distribution\n   328→1. Run `cmake --install . --config Release` to produce the shared library.  \n   329→2. Verify that `tile.manifest.json` is bundled alongside the binary.  \n   330→3. Compress to `.mbp` (MosaicBoard Package):  \n   331→   ```bash\n   332→   mbsc pack ClockTile --output ClockTile-1.0.0.mbp\n   333→   ```  \n   334→4. Upload to the *Marketplace* or distribute internally.\n   335→\n   336→---\n   337→\n   338→## 6  Troubleshooting & FAQ\n   339→**Q:** *How do I debug a tile without restarting the whole dashboard?*  \n   340→**A:** Start MosaicBoard Studio with `--live-reload`. Quick Start  \n2. Plugin ABI & Life-Cycle  \n3. Creating Your First Tile  \n4. Advanced Topics  \n   4.1 Asynchronous Data Streams  \n   4.2 The Real-Time Event Bus  \n   4.3 Secure Settings & Secrets  \n5. Packaging & Distribution  \n6. Troubleshooting & FAQ  \n\n---\n\n## 1  Quick Start\n```bash\n# 1. Scaffold a new plugin\n$ mbsc scaffold --type tile --name \"ClockTile\"\n\n# 2. Build\n$ cd ClockTile\n$ mkdir build && cd build\n$ cmake .. -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release\n$ cmake --build . --config Release\n\n# 3. Install\n$ cp libClockTile.so <MOSAICBOARD_ROOT>/plugins/\n\n# 4. Launch the dashboard\n$ mosaicboard-studio\n```\n\n> **NOTE:**  \n> *Windows users* will get `ClockTile.dll`; macOS users will get `libClockTile.dylib`.\n\n---\n\n## 2  Plugin ABI & Life-Cycle\nAll plugins must implement the C-style factory functions below.  \nThey are exported with **default** visibility so the host can `dlopen/dlsym` (or `LoadLibrary/GetProcAddress`) them without name-mangling issues.\n\n```cpp\nextern \"C\"\n{\n    MOSAIC_API IMosaicPlugin*  CreatePlugin();\n    MOSAIC_API void            DestroyPlugin(IMosaicPlugin* p);\n}\n```\n\nLife-Cycle:\n1. Host performs a **signature & version check**.  \n2. `CreatePlugin` is called once; the returned object persists for the dashboard session.  \n3. On hot-reload or shutdown, `DestroyPlugin` is invoked.  \n4. If a compilation error occurs, the host falls back to the previous working binary (atomic swap).  \n\n---\n\n## 3  Creating Your First Tile\n\nBelow is a **minimal but production-grade** example that renders a real-time UTC clock, exposes a REST endpoint, and demonstrates event-bus publishing.  Use it as a reference for your own tiles.\n\n### 3.1  Directory Layout\n```\nClockTile/\n├─ include/\n│  └─ ClockTile.hpp\n├─ src/\n│  ├─ ClockTile.cpp\n│  └─ PluginMain.cpp\n├─ tests/\n│  └─ test_ClockTile.cpp\n├─ CMakeLists.txt\n└─ tile.manifest.json\n```\n\n### 3.2  Header (`include/ClockTile.hpp`)\n```cpp\n#pragma once\n/****************************************************************************************\n * ClockTile — A reference implementation of an always-on UTC clock.\n *\n * © 2024 Your Company.  Licensed under the MIT License.\n ****************************************************************************************/\n#include <mosaic/api/ITile.hpp>          // Core rendering surface\n#include <mosaic/event/IEventBus.hpp>    // Publish/subscribe\n#include <chrono>\n#include <mutex>\n\nclass ClockTile final : public mosaic::api::ITile\n{\npublic:\n    explicit ClockTile(const mosaic::api::TileContext& ctx);\n    ~ClockTile() noexcept override = default;\n\n    // ITile overrides\n    void onAttach(mosaic::api::Surface& surface) override;\n    void onDetach() noexcept override;\n    void onTick(std::chrono::milliseconds delta) override;\n    void onHttpRequest(const mosaic::net::HttpRequest& req,\n                       mosaic::net::HttpResponse& res) override;\n\nprivate:\n    void updateModel();\n    void publishTimeChanged() const;\n\n    std::chrono::system_clock::time_point _now;\n    std::mutex                             _mutex;\n    mosaic::api::Surface*                  _surface {nullptr};\n    mosaic::event::EventBus&               _bus;\n};\n```\n\n### 3.3  Implementation (`src/ClockTile.cpp`)\n```cpp\n#include \"ClockTile.hpp\"\n#include <mosaic/log/Log.hpp>\n#include <fmt/format.h>\n\nusing namespace std::chrono_literals;\n\nClockTile::ClockTile(const mosaic::api::TileContext& ctx)\n    : ITile(ctx)\n    , _bus(ctx.eventBus)\n{\n    updateModel();\n}\n\nvoid ClockTile::onAttach(mosaic::api::Surface& surface)\n{\n    _surface = &surface;\n    surface.setMinRefreshInterval(1s);      // 1Hz refresh\n    mosaic::log::info(\"ClockTile attached.\");\n}\n\nvoid ClockTile::onDetach() noexcept\n{\n    mosaic::log::info(\"ClockTile detached.\");\n    _surface = nullptr;\n}\n\nvoid ClockTile::onTick(std::chrono::milliseconds /*delta*/)\n{\n    // Update internal state once per second.\n    std::lock_guard lk(_mutex);\n    auto newNow = std::chrono::system_clock::now();\n    if (std::chrono::floor<std::chrono::seconds>(newNow) !=\n        std::chrono::floor<std::chrono::seconds>(_now))\n    {\n        _now = newNow;\n        updateModel();\n        publishTimeChanged();\n    }\n}\n\nvoid ClockTile::onHttpRequest(const mosaic::net::HttpRequest& req,\n                              mosaic::net::HttpResponse& res)\n{\n    if (req.method() == \"GET\" && req.target() == \"/now\")\n    {\n        std::lock_guard lk(_mutex);\n        const auto iso = fmt::format(\"{:%FT%TZ}\", std::chrono::floor<std::chrono::seconds>(_now));\n        res.status(200)\n           .contentType(\"application/json\")\n           .body(fmt::format(R\"({{\"utc\":\"{}\"}})\", iso));\n    }\n    else\n    {\n        res.status(404)\n           .body(R\"({\"error\":\"endpoint_not_found\"})\");\n    }\n}\n\nvoid ClockTile::updateModel()\n{\n    if (!_surface) return;\n\n    const auto iso = fmt::format(\"{:%H:%M:%S}\", std::chrono::floor<std::chrono::seconds>(_now));\n    _surface->setProperty(\"text\", iso);\n}\n\nvoid ClockTile::publishTimeChanged() const\n{\n    _bus.publish(\"clock/updated\", [this](mosaic::event::Message& msg){\n        std::lock_guard lk(_mutex);\n        msg.set(\"timestamp\", std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(\n                                 _now.time_since_epoch()).count());\n    });\n}\n```\n\n### 3.4  Plugin Entry Point (`src/PluginMain.cpp`)\n```cpp\n#define MOSAIC_PLUGIN_EXPORTS\n#include <mosaic/plugin/PluginAPI.hpp>\n#include \"ClockTile.hpp\"\n\nusing namespace mosaic;\n\nclass ClockPlugin final : public api::IPlugin\n{\npublic:\n    const char* id()            const noexcept override { return \"com.example.clock\"; }\n    const char* name()          const noexcept override { return \"UTC Clock Tile\"; }\n    const char* version()       const noexcept override { return \"1.0.0\"; }\n    const char* sdkVersion()    const noexcept override { return MOSAIC_SDK_VERSION; }\n    const char* author()        const noexcept override { return \"Your Name\"; }\n    const char* description()   const noexcept override { return \"Displays the current UTC time.\"; }\n\n    void registerTiles(api::IRegistrar& registrar) override\n    {\n        registrar.addTile<ClockTile>(\"ClockTile\");\n    }\n};\n\n// C-style exports\nextern \"C\"\n{\n    MOSAIC_API api::IPlugin* CreatePlugin()\n    {\n        try { return new ClockPlugin(); }\n        catch (const std::exception& ex)\n        {\n            mosaic::log::error(\"Failed to construct ClockPlugin: {}\", ex.what());\n            return nullptr;\n        }\n    }\n\n    MOSAIC_API void DestroyPlugin(api::IPlugin* p)\n    {\n        delete p;\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 3.5  CMake (`CMakeLists.txt`)\n```cmake\ncmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.20)\nproject(ClockTile LANGUAGES CXX)\n\nfind_package(MosaicBoardSDK 1.4 REQUIRED)            # Provided by the main installer\nfind_package(fmt CONFIG REQUIRED)\n\nadd_library(ClockTile SHARED\n    src/ClockTile.cpp\n    src/PluginMain.cpp\n)\n\ntarget_include_directories(ClockTile PRIVATE include)\ntarget_link_libraries(ClockTile\n    PUBLIC\n        MosaicBoardSDK::Core\n        fmt::fmt\n)\n\ntarget_compile_features(ClockTile PRIVATE cxx_std_20)\ntarget_compile_definitions(ClockTile PRIVATE -DMOSAIC_PLUGIN_EXPORTS)\nset_target_properties(ClockTile PROPERTIES\n    LIBRARY_OUTPUT_DIRECTORY \"${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}\"\n    RUNTIME_OUTPUT_DIRECTORY \"${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}\"\n)\n```\n\n### 3.6  Manifest (`tile.manifest.json`)\n```json\n{\n  \"id\"          : \"com.example.clock\",\n  \"name\"        : \"UTC Clock Tile\",\n  \"version\"     : \"1.0.0\",\n  \"entryPoint\"  : \"ClockTile\",\n  \"permissions\" : [\"event_bus.publish\"],\n  \"capabilities\": {\n    \"http\": {\n      \"routes\": [\n        { \"method\": \"GET\", \"path\": \"/now\", \"description\": \"Returns current UTC time.\" }\n      ]\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### 3.7  Unit Test (`tests/test_ClockTile.cpp`)\n```cpp\n#include <gtest/gtest.h>\n#include <mosaic/testing/TileTestHarness.hpp>\n#include \"ClockTile.hpp\"\n\nTEST(ClockTile, HttpNowReturnsIso8601)\n{\n    mosaic::testing::TileTestHarness harness;\n    auto tile = harness.create<ClockTile>();\n\n    mosaic::net::HttpRequest req(\"GET\", \"/now\");\n    mosaic::net::HttpResponse res;\n    tile->onHttpRequest(req, res);\n\n    EXPECT_EQ(res.status(), 200);\n    EXPECT_EQ(res.contentType(), \"application/json\");\n    EXPECT_TRUE(res.body().find(\"\\\"utc\\\"\") != std::string::npos);\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4  Advanced Topics\n\n### 4.1  Asynchronous Data Streams\nYour tile can spawn background tasks via `ctx.executor`, which is a thread-pool configured for *short-lived, non-blocking* jobs. Remember to call `_surface->requestRepaint()` from the main thread only.\n\n### 4.2  The Real-Time Event Bus\nUse topic naming conventions: `namespace/subsystem/action`.  \nEvents can be **sticky** (retained) or **volatile** (fire-and-forget). Declare them in your manifest for subscriber auto-completion inside the Visual Composer.\n\n### 4.3  Secure Settings & Secrets\nSensitive data (API keys, DB credentials) should **never** be hard-coded.  \nUse the Settings service:\n\n```cpp\nauto secret = ctx.settings().getSecret(\"my_service.api_key\");\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5  Packaging & Distribution\n1. Run `cmake --install . --config Release` to produce the shared library.  \n2. Verify that `tile.manifest.json` is bundled alongside the binary.  \n3. Compress to `.mbp` (MosaicBoard Package):  \n   ```bash\n   mbsc pack ClockTile --output ClockTile-1.0.0.mbp\n   ```  \n4. Upload to the *Marketplace* or distribute internally.\n\n---\n\n## 6  Troubleshooting & FAQ\n**Q:** *How do I debug a tile without restarting the whole dashboard?*  \n**A:** Start MosaicBoard Studio with `--live-reload`. std::filesystem::path(\"./plugins\"))\n   103→    {\n   104→        if (!std::filesystem::exists(directory))\n   105→            return 0;\n   106→\n   107→        std::size_t loaded = 0;\n   108→  82→\n    83→    // Generates smooth pseudo-random value between 0 and 1 for a given position.\n    84→    // Uses simple linear interpolation of two hashed lattice points.\n    85→    float operator()(float x)\n    86→    {\n    87→        const int   xi0 = static_cast<int>(std::floor(x));\n    88→        const int   xi1 = xi0 + 1;\n    89→        const float xf  = x - const float t  = xf * xf * (3.f - 2.f * xf);\n    96→        return lerp(v0, v1, t);\n    97→    }\n    98→\n    99→private:\n   100→    float lattice(int xi)\n   101→    {\n   102→ static_cast<int>(std::floor(x));\n        const int   xi1 = xi0 + 1;\n        const float xf  = x - static_cast<float>(xi0);\n\n        const float v0 = lattice(xi0);\n        const float v1 = lattice(xi1);\n\n        // Smoothstep interpolation\n        const float t  = xf * xf * (3.f - 2.f * xf);\n        return lerp(v0, v1, t);\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    float lattice(int xi)\n    {\n        std::mt19937 rng(static_cast<std::uint32_t>(xi) ^ m_seed);\n        return m_distribution(rng);\n    }\n\n    static float lerp(float a, float b, float t) { return a + (b - a) * t; }\n\n http::error::end_of_stream)\n                        spdlog::warn(\"Read error: {}\", ec.message());\n                    self->doClose();\n                    return;\n                }\n                self->handleRequest();\n            });\n        }\n\n        void handleRequest()\n        {\n            auto res          = reqHandler_(std::move(req_));\n            auto self         = shared_from_this();\n            auto sp           = std::make_shared<string_res>(std::move(res));\n            stream_.asyncWrite(*sp, [self, sp](boost::system::error_code ec, std::size_t) {\n                if (ec)\n                {\n                    spdlog::warn(\"Write error: {}\", ec.message());\n                    self->doClose();\n                    return;\n                }\n                if (sp->need_eof()) { self->doClose(); }\n                else { self->doRead(); }\n            });\n        }\n\n        void doClose()\n        {\n            boost::system::error_code ec;\n            stream_.shutdown(ec);   // For SSL\n            stream_.socket().shutdown(tcp::socket::shutdown_send, ec);\n        }\n\n        /* --------------------- Helper wrapper for SSL --------------------- */\n\n        struct BeastStream\n        {\n            using PlainStream   = boost::beast::tcp_stream;\n            using SSLStream     = boost::beast::ssl_stream<PlainStream>;\n\n            BeastStream(tcp::socket &&sock, boost::asio::ssl::context *sslCtx)\n                : plain_{std::move(sock)}, ssl_{std::move(plain_), *sslCtx}, useSSL_{sslCtx != nullptr}\n            {\n            }\n\n            bool isSSL() const { return useSSL_; }\n\n            template <typename MutableBufferSequence, typename ReadHandler>\n            void asyncRead(MutableBufferSequence const &buffers, ReadHandler &&handler)\n            {\n                if (useSSL_) { http::async_read(ssl_, buffer_, req_, std::forward<ReadHandler>(handler)); }\n                else { http::async_read(plain_, buffer_, req_, std::forward<ReadHandler>(handler)); }\n            }\n\n            template <typename Body, typename Fields, typename WriteHandler>\n            void asyncWrite(http::message<true, Body, Fields> &msg, WriteHandler &&handler)\n            {\n                if (useSSL_) { http::async_write(ssl_, msg, std::forward<WriteHandler>(handler)); }\n                else { http::async_write(plain_, msg, std::forward<WriteHandler>(handler)); }\n            }\n\n            template <typename HandshakeHandler>\n            void asyncHandshakeServer(HandshakeHandler &&handler)\n            {\n                ssl_.async_handshake(boost::asio::ssl::stream_base::server, std::forward<HandshakeHandler>(handler));\n            }\n\n            void shutdown(boost::system::error_code &ec)\n            {\n                if (useSSL_) ssl_.shutdown(ec);\n            }\n\n            tcp::socket &socket() { return useSSL_ ? ssl_.next_layer().socket() : plain_.socket(); }\n\n            boost::beast::flat_buffer buffer_;\n            string_req                req_;\n\n        private:\n            PlainStream plain_;\n            SSLStream   ssl_;\n            bool        useSSL_;\n        };\n\n        BeastStream stream_;\n        string_req  req_;\n        ReqHandler  reqHandler_;\n    };\n};\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                                Server facade                               */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nServer::Server(const Options &opt) : pImpl_{std::make_unique<Impl>(opt)} {}\n\nServer::~Server() = default;\n\nvoid Server::start()               { pImpl_->start(); }\nvoid Server::stop()                { pImpl_->stop(); }\nbool Server::isRunning() const     { return pImpl_->isRunning(); }\nvoid Server::reloadPlugins()       { pImpl_->reloadPlugins(); }\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                                 Factory API                                */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nServer::Options Server::Options::withDefaults()\n{\n    Options o;\n    o.address         = \"0.0.0.0\";\n    o.port            = 8443;\n    o.useSSL          = true;\n    o.certFile        = \"certs/server.crt\";\n    o.keyFile         = \"certs/server.key\";\n    o.threadCount     = std::max<std::size_t>(2, std::thread::hardware_concurrency());\n    o.pluginDirectory = \"plugins\";\n    return o;\n}\n\n}  // namespace mb::core","numLines":538,"startLine":1,"totalLines":538}}}
