{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-5-20251101","id":"msg_015bNRxpPnMofCyGrTkMyxpd","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01H8gEdTsLdZtRPttof4JYTx","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/logs/agent/solution.md","content":"# Technical Design Document: Plugin API Endpoint Registration and EventBus Integration\n\n## Executive Summary\n\nThis document outlines the architectural extension to MosaicBoard Studio's plugin system to enable plugins to:\n1. Register custom HTTP API endpoints\n2. Subscribe to system-wide events via the central EventBus\n\nThe design maintains backward compatibility with existing `ITile`-based plugins while introducing a new, more comprehensive `IPlugin` interface that serves as the main entry point for enhanced plugin capabilities.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Current State Analysis\n\n### 1.1 Plugin System Architecture Overview\n\nThe current plugin system is centered around the **Tile** concept - visual components that can be hot-swapped into dashboards. The key classes involved are:\n\n#### Key Classes and Files:\n\n| Class/File | Location | Responsibility |\n|------------|----------|----------------|\n| `PluginManager` | `src/core/PluginManager.h`, `src/core/PluginManager.cpp` | Central runtime-loader for plugins; handles discovery, loading, unloading, and hot-reloading |\n| `IPlugin` | `src/core/PluginManager.h:47-59` | Basic plugin interface with `name()`, `version()`, `start()`, `stop()` methods |\n| `ITile` | `src/interfaces/ITile.h` | Visual tile interface with lifecycle, rendering, and event handling methods |\n| `EventBus` | `src/core/EventBus.h` | Thread-safe publish/subscribe message broker |\n| `Server` | `src/core/Server.h`, `src/core/Server.cpp` | HTTP/WebSocket server with `Router` class for route management |\n\n### 1.2 Current Plugin Loading Mechanism\n\nThe `PluginManager` uses dynamic library loading (`dlopen`/`dlsym` on POSIX, `LoadLibrary`/`GetProcAddress` on Windows) to discover and load plugins:\n\n**From `src/core/PluginManager.cpp:196-202`:**\n```cpp\n// Resolve factory functions\nauto createFn  = loadSymbol<CreatePluginFn>(handle->dlHandle, CREATE_FN_NAME);\nauto destroyFn = loadSymbol<DestroyPluginFn>(handle->dlHandle, DESTROY_FN_NAME);\n\n// Create plugin instance\nstd::unique_ptr<IPlugin> pluginPtr(createFn());\n```\n\nThe current entry point symbols expected from plugins are:\n- `mbsCreatePlugin` - Factory function returning `IPlugin*`\n- `mbsDestroyPlugin` - Destruction function\n\n**Current Plugin Lifecycle (from `src/core/PluginManager.cpp:224-235`):**\n1. Plugin library is opened via `dlopen`\n2. Factory symbols are resolved\n3. Plugin instance is created via factory\n4. Metadata is loaded (from sidecar JSON or `IPlugin::metadata()`)\n5. Plugin subscribes to EventBus via `instance->subscribe(_eventBus)`\n6. Telemetry event is emitted\n7. Handle is stored in `_handles` vector\n\n### 1.3 Current Interface Limitations\n\nThe existing `IPlugin` interface (`src/core/PluginManager.h:47-59`) is minimal:\n\n```cpp\nclass IPlugin\n{\npublic:\n    virtual ~IPlugin() = default;\n    virtual const char* name()    const noexcept = 0;\n    virtual const char* version() const noexcept = 0;\n    virtual void start() = 0;\n    virtual void stop()  = 0;\n};\n```\n\nWhile `ITile` (`src/interfaces/ITile.h:95-166`) provides visual component capabilities, neither interface supports:\n- HTTP route registration\n- Direct EventBus access during initialization\n- Explicit lifecycle phases for backend integration\n\n---\n\n## 2. API Endpoint Registration Design\n\n### 2.1 Design Goals\n\n1. **Decoupling**: Plugins should not receive direct access to the `Server` instance\n2. **Security**: Plugin routes should be namespaced to prevent conflicts and unauthorized access\n3. **Discoverability**: The system should know all plugin-registered routes for documentation/introspection\n4. **Thread Safety**: Route registration must be thread-safe\n\n### 2.2 Proposed Mechanism\n\n#### 2.2.1 Route Definition Structure\n\nCreate a new structure for plugins to declare their routes:\n\n```cpp\n// In new file: src/interfaces/IPluginRoute.h\n\nnamespace mbs::plugin {\n\nstruct HttpRoute {\n    std::string method;        // \"GET\", \"POST\", \"PUT\", \"DELETE\"\n    std::string path;          // Relative path, e.g., \"/status\" or \"/data/:id\"\n    std::string description;   // For documentation/OpenAPI generation\n\n    using Handler = std::function<void(const HttpRequest&, HttpResponse&)>;\n    Handler handler;\n};\n\nusing RouteCollection = std::vector<HttpRoute>;\n\n} // namespace mbs::plugin\n```\n\n#### 2.2.2 Integration with Server Router\n\nThe `Router` class in `src/core/Server.h:62-98` already supports dynamic route registration:\n\n```cpp\nvoid add(http::verb method, std::string_view pattern, HttpHandler cb)\n{\n    std::unique_lock lock(mutex_);\n    routes_.emplace_back(method, std::regex{std::string(pattern)}, std::move(cb));\n}\n```\n\nThis existing mechanism can be leveraged by the `PluginManager`.\n\n#### 2.2.3 PluginManager as Route Mediator\n\nThe `PluginManager` should act as a mediator between plugins and the `Server`:\n\n```cpp\n// Additions to PluginManager class\n\nclass PluginManager {\npublic:\n    // Inject server reference during construction\n    PluginManager(EventBus& bus, Server& server);\n\n    // Register routes for a specific plugin (called internally during loadPlugin)\n    void registerPluginRoutes(const std::string& pluginId, const RouteCollection& routes);\n\n    // Unregister routes when plugin is unloaded\n    void unregisterPluginRoutes(const std::string& pluginId);\n\nprivate:\n    Server& _server;\n    std::unordered_map<std::string, std::vector<std::string>> _pluginRoutes; // pluginId -> registered paths\n};\n```\n\n#### 2.2.4 Route Namespacing\n\nAll plugin routes should be automatically prefixed with `/api/v1/plugins/<plugin-id>/`:\n\n```cpp\nvoid PluginManager::registerPluginRoutes(const std::string& pluginId, const RouteCollection& routes)\n{\n    const std::string prefix = \"/api/v1/plugins/\" + pluginId;\n\n    for (const auto& route : routes) {\n        const std::string fullPath = prefix + route.path;\n\n        // Convert string method to http::verb\n        auto verb = methodToVerb(route.method);\n\n        // Register with server, wrapping handler with error boundary\n        _server.router().add(verb, fullPath,\n            [handler = route.handler](const HttpRequest& req, HttpResponse& res) {\n                try {\n                    handler(req, res);\n                } catch (const std::exception& ex) {\n                    res.result(http::status::internal_server_error);\n                    res.body() = R\"({\"error\":\")\" + std::string(ex.what()) + R\"(\"})\";\n                }\n            });\n\n        _pluginRoutes[pluginId].push_back(fullPath);\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 2.3 Server Class Modifications\n\nThe `Server` class (`src/core/Server.h`) needs minor modifications:\n\n1. **Expose Router Reference**: Add a method to access the router for the `PluginManager`:\n\n```cpp\n// In Server class\nRouter& router() noexcept { return router_; }\nconst Router& router() const noexcept { return router_; }\n```\n\n2. **Route Removal Support**: Add capability to remove routes (for hot-reload):\n\n```cpp\n// In Router class (src/core/Server.h:62-98)\nvoid remove(std::string_view pattern)\n{\n    std::unique_lock lock(mutex_);\n    auto it = std::remove_if(routes_.begin(), routes_.end(),\n        [&pattern](const Route& r) {\n            // Compare compiled regex pattern\n            return r.patternStr == pattern;\n        });\n    routes_.erase(it, routes_.end());\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. EventBus Integration Design\n\n### 3.1 Current EventBus API\n\nThe EventBus (`src/core/EventBus.h:180-319`) already provides a robust publish/subscribe API:\n\n```cpp\ntemplate <typename EventT>\n[[nodiscard]] SubscriptionToken subscribe(\n    std::function<void(const EventT&)> callback,\n    EventDelivery delivery = EventDelivery::Async);\n\ntemplate <typename EventT, typename... Args>\nvoid publish(Args&&... args);\n```\n\nKey features:\n- Type-safe subscriptions using template parameters\n- RAII `SubscriptionToken` for automatic unsubscription\n- Sync and Async delivery modes\n- Thread pool for async dispatch\n\n### 3.2 Plugin Access to EventBus\n\n#### 3.2.1 Context Injection Pattern\n\nThe recommended approach is to inject the `EventBus` reference during plugin initialization via a context object:\n\n```cpp\n// In new file: src/interfaces/PluginContext.h\n\nnamespace mbs::plugin {\n\nstruct PluginContext {\n    EventBus&              eventBus;       // Reference to central event bus\n    const nlohmann::json&  configuration;  // Plugin-specific configuration\n    Logger*                logger;         // Optional logger instance\n    std::filesystem::path  dataDirectory;  // Plugin's data storage path\n\n    // Read-only access to system information\n    std::string            pluginId;\n    Version                hostVersion;\n};\n\n} // namespace mbs::plugin\n```\n\n#### 3.2.2 PluginManager Modifications for EventBus Access\n\nThe `PluginManager` already has a reference to the `EventBus` (from `src/core/PluginManager.cpp:140`):\n\n```cpp\nPluginManager::PluginManager(EventBus& bus) : _eventBus(bus)\n```\n\nCurrently, plugins subscribe via `handle->instance->subscribe(_eventBus)` (line 226). This should be enhanced to pass a `PluginContext` during initialization:\n\n```cpp\n// Modified loadPlugin method\nvoid PluginManager::loadPlugin(const std::filesystem::path& libPath)\n{\n    // ... existing library loading code ...\n\n    // Build plugin context\n    PluginContext ctx {\n        .eventBus      = _eventBus,\n        .configuration = loadPluginConfig(pluginId),\n        .logger        = Logger::acquire(pluginId),\n        .dataDirectory = getPluginDataDir(pluginId),\n        .pluginId      = pluginId,\n        .hostVersion   = currentVersion()\n    };\n\n    // Initialize plugin with context (new interface method)\n    pluginPtr->initialize(ctx);\n\n    // ... rest of loading code ...\n}\n```\n\n### 3.3 System Events for Plugins\n\nPlugins should be able to subscribe to standardized system events:\n\n```cpp\n// In new file: src/core/SystemEvents.h\n\nnamespace mbs::events {\n\nstruct UserCreated {\n    std::string userId;\n    std::string email;\n    std::chrono::system_clock::time_point timestamp;\n};\n\nstruct PaymentSuccessful {\n    std::string transactionId;\n    std::string userId;\n    double amount;\n    std::string currency;\n};\n\nstruct DashboardSaved {\n    std::string dashboardId;\n    std::string ownerId;\n};\n\nstruct PluginLoaded {\n    std::string pluginId;\n    Version version;\n};\n\nstruct PluginUnloaded {\n    std::string pluginId;\n};\n\n} // namespace mbs::events\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. New Plugin Interface Design\n\n### 4.1 Interface Design Decision\n\n**Recommendation**: Create a new, more comprehensive `IPlugin` interface rather than modifying `ITile`.\n\n**Justification**:\n1. **Separation of Concerns**: `ITile` is specifically for visual dashboard components; backend integration is a different responsibility\n2. **Backward Compatibility**: Existing tile-only plugins continue to work unchanged\n3. **Single Responsibility**: The new `IPlugin` orchestrates plugin lifecycle and capabilities; `ITile` remains focused on rendering\n4. **Extensibility**: New plugin types (e.g., data providers, authenticators) can implement `IPlugin` without `ITile`\n\n### 4.2 Proposed IPlugin Interface\n\n```cpp\n// In new file: src/interfaces/IPlugin.h\n\n#pragma once\n\n#include <chrono>\n#include <memory>\n#include <string>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include \"PluginContext.h\"\n#include \"IPluginRoute.h\"\n#include \"ITile.h\"\n\nnamespace mbs::plugin {\n\n/**\n * API version for the new plugin interface.\n * Increment on breaking changes.\n */\nconstexpr std::uint32_t PLUGIN_API_VERSION = 0x0200;  // 2.0.0\n\n/**\n * Plugin metadata descriptor.\n */\nstruct PluginDescriptor {\n    std::string id;           // Globally unique identifier (e.g., \"com.acme.analytics\")\n    std::string name;         // Human-readable name\n    std::string version;      // Semantic version string\n    std::string author;       // Author/organization\n    std::string description;  // Brief description\n    std::string license;      // License identifier (e.g., \"MIT\", \"Apache-2.0\")\n\n    // Capability flags\n    bool providesRoutes  = false;\n    bool providesTiles   = false;\n    bool providesEvents  = false;\n};\n\n/**\n * Tile factory registration structure.\n */\nstruct TileFactory {\n    std::string tileTypeId;   // Unique identifier for this tile type\n    std::string displayName;  // Human-readable name\n    std::function<std::unique_ptr<ITile>()> factory;\n};\n\n/**\n * Main plugin interface for the enhanced plugin system.\n *\n * All shared libraries must expose factory functions with signatures:\n *   extern \"C\" IPlugin* mbsCreatePlugin();\n *   extern \"C\" void mbsDestroyPlugin(IPlugin*);\n *   extern \"C\" std::uint32_t mbsPluginApiVersion();\n */\nclass IPlugin {\npublic:\n    virtual ~IPlugin() = default;\n\n    // =========================================================================\n    // Metadata\n    // =========================================================================\n\n    /**\n     * Returns the plugin descriptor containing metadata.\n     * Must be valid immediately after construction.\n     */\n    [[nodiscard]] virtual const PluginDescriptor& descriptor() const noexcept = 0;\n\n    // =========================================================================\n    // Lifecycle\n    // =========================================================================\n\n    /**\n     * Called once after construction to initialize the plugin.\n     * The plugin should:\n     *   - Store a reference to the EventBus from context\n     *   - Subscribe to any events it needs\n     *   - Perform any one-time setup\n     *\n     * May throw to indicate initialization failure.\n     *\n     * @param context Runtime context with service references\n     */\n    virtual void initialize(const PluginContext& context) = 0;\n\n    /**\n     * Called when the plugin should clean up and prepare for unloading.\n     * Must be idempotent and never throw.\n     * All EventBus subscriptions should be released here.\n     */\n    virtual void shutdown() noexcept = 0;\n\n    // =========================================================================\n    // HTTP Route Registration\n    // =========================================================================\n\n    /**\n     * Returns the collection of HTTP routes this plugin wants to register.\n     * Called once after initialize().\n     * Routes will be automatically prefixed with /api/v1/plugins/<plugin-id>/.\n     *\n     * @return Collection of route definitions (may be empty)\n     */\n    [[nodiscard]] virtual RouteCollection getRoutes() const { return {}; }\n\n    // =========================================================================\n    // Tile Factories\n    // =========================================================================\n\n    /**\n     * Returns the tile factories this plugin provides.\n     * Called once after initialize().\n     *\n     * @return Collection of tile factories (may be empty)\n     */\n    [[nodiscard]] virtual std::vector<TileFactory> getTileFactories() const { return {}; }\n\n    // =========================================================================\n    // Event Handling (Optional)\n    // =========================================================================\n\n    /**\n     * Called when a subscribed event is received.\n     * Default implementation does nothing.\n     *\n     * Note: Plugins should prefer using the typed EventBus::subscribe() API\n     * directly for type-safe event handling. This method is provided for\n     * generic event handling scenarios.\n     */\n    virtual void onEvent(const std::string& topic, const nlohmann::json& payload) {}\n};\n\n/**\n * Macro for declaring a plugin implementation with proper C exports.\n */\n#define DECLARE_MOSAIC_PLUGIN(PLUGIN_IMPL)                                      \\\n    extern \"C\" {                                                                \\\n        [[maybe_unused]]                                                        \\\n        ::mbs::plugin::IPlugin* mbsCreatePlugin()                               \\\n        {                                                                       \\\n            return new PLUGIN_IMPL();                                           \\\n        }                                                                       \\\n                                                                                \\\n        [[maybe_unused]]                                                        \\\n        void mbsDestroyPlugin(::mbs::plugin::IPlugin* ptr) noexcept             \\\n        {                                                                       \\\n            delete ptr;                                                         \\\n        }                                                                       \\\n                                                                                \\\n        [[maybe_unused]]                                                        \\\n        std::uint32_t mbsPluginApiVersion() noexcept                            \\\n        {                                                                       \\\n            return ::mbs::plugin::PLUGIN_API_VERSION;                           \\\n        }                                                                       \\\n    }\n\n} // namespace mbs::plugin\n```\n\n### 4.3 Example Plugin Implementation\n\n```cpp\n// Example: Analytics Plugin with HTTP endpoint and EventBus subscription\n\n#include <mbs/plugin/IPlugin.h>\n#include <mbs/core/EventBus.h>\n#include <mbs/events/SystemEvents.h>\n\nclass AnalyticsPlugin final : public mbs::plugin::IPlugin {\npublic:\n    const mbs::plugin::PluginDescriptor& descriptor() const noexcept override {\n        static const mbs::plugin::PluginDescriptor desc {\n            .id          = \"com.example.analytics\",\n            .name        = \"Analytics Plugin\",\n            .version     = \"1.0.0\",\n            .author      = \"Example Corp\",\n            .description = \"Tracks user events and provides analytics API\",\n            .license     = \"MIT\",\n            .providesRoutes = true,\n            .providesTiles  = false,\n            .providesEvents = true\n        };\n        return desc;\n    }\n\n    void initialize(const mbs::plugin::PluginContext& ctx) override {\n        _eventBus = &ctx.eventBus;\n        _pluginId = ctx.pluginId;\n\n        // Subscribe to user creation events\n        _userCreatedToken = _eventBus->subscribe<mbs::events::UserCreated>(\n            [this](const auto& event) {\n                trackUserCreation(event);\n            });\n\n        // Subscribe to payment events\n        _paymentToken = _eventBus->subscribe<mbs::events::PaymentSuccessful>(\n            [this](const auto& event) {\n                trackPayment(event);\n            });\n    }\n\n    void shutdown() noexcept override {\n        // Tokens auto-unsubscribe on destruction, but explicit release is cleaner\n        _userCreatedToken = {};\n        _paymentToken = {};\n    }\n\n    mbs::plugin::RouteCollection getRoutes() const override {\n        return {\n            {\n                .method = \"GET\",\n                .path = \"/stats\",\n                .description = \"Get analytics statistics\",\n                .handler = [this](const auto& req, auto& res) {\n                    handleGetStats(req, res);\n                }\n            },\n            {\n                .method = \"POST\",\n                .path = \"/track\",\n                .description = \"Track custom event\",\n                .handler = [this](const auto& req, auto& res) {\n                    handleTrackEvent(req, res);\n                }\n            }\n        };\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    void trackUserCreation(const mbs::events::UserCreated& event) {\n        // Store analytics data\n    }\n\n    void trackPayment(const mbs::events::PaymentSuccessful& event) {\n        // Store payment analytics\n    }\n\n    void handleGetStats(const HttpRequest& req, HttpResponse& res) {\n        res.body() = R\"({\"totalUsers\": 1234, \"totalRevenue\": 5678.90})\";\n        res.set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\");\n    }\n\n    void handleTrackEvent(const HttpRequest& req, HttpResponse& res) {\n        // Process custom tracking\n        res.result(http::status::accepted);\n    }\n\n    mbs::core::EventBus* _eventBus = nullptr;\n    std::string _pluginId;\n    mbs::core::SubscriptionToken _userCreatedToken;\n    mbs::core::SubscriptionToken _paymentToken;\n};\n\nDECLARE_MOSAIC_PLUGIN(AnalyticsPlugin)\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Modified Plugin Lifecycle in PluginManager\n\n### 5.1 Updated Loading Sequence\n\n```cpp\nvoid PluginManager::loadPlugin(const std::filesystem::path& libPath)\n{\n    // 1. Open library\n    auto handle = std::make_unique<PluginHandle>();\n    handle->dlHandle = openLibrary(libPath);\n\n    // 2. Check API version compatibility\n    auto versionFn = loadSymbol<VersionFn>(handle->dlHandle, \"mbsPluginApiVersion\");\n    if (versionFn && versionFn() != PLUGIN_API_VERSION) {\n        throw MBSException(\"Incompatible plugin API version\");\n    }\n\n    // 3. Resolve factory functions\n    auto createFn = loadSymbol<CreatePluginFn>(handle->dlHandle, \"mbsCreatePlugin\");\n    auto destroyFn = loadSymbol<DestroyPluginFn>(handle->dlHandle, \"mbsDestroyPlugin\");\n\n    // 4. Create plugin instance\n    std::unique_ptr<IPlugin> pluginPtr(createFn(), destroyFn);\n\n    // 5. Build context\n    const auto& desc = pluginPtr->descriptor();\n    PluginContext ctx {\n        .eventBus      = _eventBus,\n        .configuration = loadPluginConfig(desc.id),\n        .logger        = Logger::acquire(desc.id),\n        .dataDirectory = getPluginDataDir(desc.id),\n        .pluginId      = desc.id,\n        .hostVersion   = currentVersion()\n    };\n\n    // 6. Initialize plugin (may subscribe to events)\n    pluginPtr->initialize(ctx);\n\n    // 7. Register HTTP routes (if any)\n    if (desc.providesRoutes) {\n        auto routes = pluginPtr->getRoutes();\n        registerPluginRoutes(desc.id, routes);\n    }\n\n    // 8. Register tile factories (if any)\n    if (desc.providesTiles) {\n        auto factories = pluginPtr->getTileFactories();\n        for (const auto& factory : factories) {\n            _tileRegistry.registerFactory(desc.id, factory);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // 9. Store handle\n    handle->instance = std::move(pluginPtr);\n    handle->metadata = buildMetadata(desc, libPath);\n    handle->loadTimestamp = clock::now();\n\n    // 10. Emit telemetry\n    Telemetry::instance().emit(\"plugin.loaded\", {\n        {\"id\", desc.id},\n        {\"version\", desc.version},\n        {\"providesRoutes\", desc.providesRoutes},\n        {\"providesTiles\", desc.providesTiles}\n    });\n\n    _handles.emplace_back(std::move(handle));\n}\n```\n\n### 5.2 Updated Unloading Sequence\n\n```cpp\nvoid PluginManager::unloadPlugin(const std::string& pluginId)\n{\n    auto it = findPluginById(pluginId);\n    if (it == _handles.end()) {\n        throw MBSException(\"Plugin not found: \" + pluginId);\n    }\n\n    auto& handle = *it;\n    const auto& desc = handle->instance->descriptor();\n\n    // 1. Unregister HTTP routes\n    if (desc.providesRoutes) {\n        unregisterPluginRoutes(pluginId);\n    }\n\n    // 2. Unregister tile factories\n    if (desc.providesTiles) {\n        _tileRegistry.unregisterFactories(pluginId);\n    }\n\n    // 3. Shutdown plugin (releases EventBus subscriptions)\n    handle->instance->shutdown();\n\n    // 4. Destroy instance\n    handle->instance.reset();\n\n    // 5. Close library\n    closeLibrary(handle->dlHandle);\n\n    // 6. Remove from collection\n    _handles.erase(it);\n\n    // 7. Emit telemetry\n    Telemetry::instance().emit(\"plugin.unloaded\", {{\"id\", pluginId}});\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 6. Summary of Required Changes\n\n### 6.1 New Files to Create\n\n| File | Purpose |\n|------|---------|\n| `src/interfaces/IPlugin.h` | New comprehensive plugin interface |\n| `src/interfaces/PluginContext.h` | Context object for plugin initialization |\n| `src/interfaces/IPluginRoute.h` | HTTP route definition structures |\n| `src/core/SystemEvents.h` | Standardized system event types |\n\n### 6.2 Files to Modify\n\n| File | Changes |\n|------|---------|\n| `src/core/PluginManager.h` | Add `Server&` reference, route registration methods |\n| `src/core/PluginManager.cpp` | Update `loadPlugin`/`unloadPlugin` with new lifecycle |\n| `src/core/Server.h` | Expose `router()` accessor, add route removal to `Router` |\n| `src/core/Server.cpp` | Implement route removal functionality |\n\n### 6.3 Backward Compatibility\n\nThe existing `ITile` interface (`src/interfaces/ITile.h`) remains unchanged. Plugins that only provide tiles can continue using the simpler tile-factory registration pattern through the new `IPlugin::getTileFactories()` method.\n\n---\n\n## 7. Security Considerations\n\n1. **Route Namespacing**: All plugin routes are automatically prefixed, preventing namespace collisions and unauthorized access to core routes\n2. **Error Boundaries**: Plugin route handlers are wrapped in try-catch to prevent plugin exceptions from crashing the server\n3. **Capability Flags**: Plugins must declare their capabilities, enabling host-level permission checks\n4. **API Version Checking**: Incompatible plugins are rejected at load time\n\n---\n\n## 8. Conclusion\n\nThis design provides a clean architectural extension to MosaicBoard Studio's plugin system that:\n\n1. **Maintains Decoupling**: Plugins interact with the system through well-defined interfaces; the `PluginManager` mediates between plugins and core components\n2. **Preserves Backward Compatibility**: Existing `ITile`-only plugins continue to work\n3. **Enables Deep Integration**: Plugins can now register HTTP endpoints and subscribe to system events\n4. **Follows Established Patterns**: The design leverages existing patterns in the codebase (factory functions, RAII tokens, context injection)\n\nThe key insight is that `IPlugin` serves as the new \"main entry point\" for plugins, while `ITile` remains focused solely on visual rendering - a clean separation of concerns that scales well as plugin capabilities expand.\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":105,"cache_read_input_tokens":91346,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":105,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":25,"service_tier":"standard"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"c4b125a4-0d21-4867-a386-b7da19589c46","uuid":"1e0b75c2-5593-4e16-9b68-d1bbfd3169df"}

Register custom HTTP API endpoints\n2. Subscribe to system-wide events via the central EventBus\n\nThe design maintains backward compatibility with existing `ITile`-based plugins while introducing a new, more comprehensive `IPlugin` interface that serves as the main entry point for enhanced plugin capabilities.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Current State Analysis\n\n### 1.1 Plugin System Architecture Overview\n\nThe current plugin system is centered around the **Tile** concept - visual components that can be hot-swapped into dashboards. The key classes involved are:\n\n#### Key Classes and Files:\n\n| Class/File | Location | Responsibility |\n|------------|----------|----------------|\n| `PluginManager` | `src/core/PluginManager.h`, `src/core/PluginManager.cpp` | Central runtime-loader for plugins; handles discovery, loading, unloading, and hot-reloading |\n| `IPlugin` | `src/core/PluginManager.h:47-59` | Basic plugin interface with `name()`, `version()`, `start()`, `stop()` methods |\n| `ITile` | `src/interfaces/ITile.h` | Visual tile interface with lifecycle, rendering, and event handling methods |\n| `EventBus` | `src/core/EventBus.h` | Thread-safe publish/subscribe message broker |\n| `Server` | `src/core/Server.h`, `src/core/Server.cpp` | HTTP/WebSocket server with `Router` class for route management |\n\n### 1.2 Current Plugin Loading Mechanism\n\nThe `PluginManager` uses dynamic library loading (`dlopen`/`dlsym` on POSIX, `LoadLibrary`/`GetProcAddress` on Windows) to discover and load plugins:\n\n**From `src/core/PluginManager.cpp:196-202`:**\n```cpp\n// Resolve factory functions\nauto createFn  = loadSymbol<CreatePluginFn>(handle->dlHandle, CREATE_FN_NAME);\nauto destroyFn = loadSymbol<DestroyPluginFn>(handle->dlHandle, DESTROY_FN_NAME);\n\n// Create plugin instance\nstd::unique_ptr<IPlugin> pluginPtr(createFn());\n```\n\nThe current entry point symbols expected from plugins are:\n- `mbsCreatePlugin` - Factory function returning `IPlugin*`\n- `mbsDestroyPlugin` - Destruction function\n\n**Current Plugin Lifecycle (from `src/core/PluginManager.cpp:224-235`):**\n1. Plugin library is opened via `dlopen`\n2. Factory symbols are resolved\n3. Plugin instance is created via factory\n4. Metadata is loaded (from sidecar JSON or `IPlugin::metadata()`)\n5. Plugin subscribes to EventBus via `instance->subscribe(_eventBus)`\n6. Telemetry event is emitted\n7. Handle is stored in `_handles` vector\n\n### 1.3 Current Interface Limitations\n\nThe existing `IPlugin` interface (`src/core/PluginManager.h:47-59`) is minimal:\n\n```cpp\nclass IPlugin\n{\npublic:\n    virtual ~IPlugin() = default;\n    virtual const char* name()    const noexcept = 0;\n    virtual const char* version() const noexcept = 0;\n    virtual void start() = 0;\n    virtual void stop()  = 0;\n};\n```\n\nWhile `ITile` (`src/interfaces/ITile.h:95-166`) provides visual component capabilities, neither interface supports:\n- HTTP route registration\n- Direct EventBus access during initialization\n- Explicit lifecycle phases for backend integration\n\n---\n\n## 2. API Endpoint Registration Design\n\n### 2.1 Design Goals\n\n1. **Decoupling**: Plugins should not receive direct access to the `Server` instance\n2. **Security**: Plugin routes should be namespaced to prevent conflicts and unauthorized access\n3. **Discoverability**: The system should know all plugin-registered routes for documentation/introspection\n4. **Thread Safety**: Route registration must be thread-safe\n\n### 2.2 Proposed Mechanism\n\n#### 2.2.1 Route Definition Structure\n\nCreate a new structure for plugins to declare their routes:\n\n```cpp\n// In new file: src/interfaces/IPluginRoute.h\n\nnamespace mbs::plugin {\n\nstruct HttpRoute {\n    std::string method;        // \"GET\", \"POST\", \"PUT\", \"DELETE\"\n    std::string path;          // Relative path, e.g., \"/status\" or \"/data/:id\"\n    std::string description;   // For documentation/OpenAPI generation\n\n    using Handler = std::function<void(const HttpRequest&, HttpResponse&)>;\n    Handler handler;\n};\n\nusing RouteCollection = std::vector<HttpRoute>;\n\n} // namespace mbs::plugin\n```\n\n#### 2.2.2 Integration with Server Router\n\nThe `Router` class in `src/core/Server.h:62-98` already supports dynamic route registration:\n\n```cpp\nvoid add(http::verb method, std::string_view pattern, HttpHandler cb)\n{\n    std::unique_lock lock(mutex_);\n    routes_.emplace_back(method, std::regex{std::string(pattern)}, std::move(cb));\n}\n```\n\nThis existing mechanism can be leveraged by the `PluginManager`.\n\n#### 2.2.3 PluginManager as Route Mediator\n\nThe `PluginManager` should act as a mediator between plugins and the `Server`:\n\n```cpp\n// Additions to PluginManager class\n\nclass PluginManager {\npublic:\n    // Inject server reference during construction\n    PluginManager(EventBus& bus, Server& server);\n\n    // Register routes for a specific plugin (called internally during loadPlugin)\n    void registerPluginRoutes(const std::string& pluginId, const RouteCollection& routes);\n\n    // Unregister routes when plugin is unloaded\n    void unregisterPluginRoutes(const std::string& pluginId);\n\nprivate:\n    Server& _server;\n    std::unordered_map<std::string, std::vector<std::string>> _pluginRoutes; // pluginId -> registered paths\n};\n```\n\n#### 2.2.4 Route Namespacing\n\nAll plugin routes should be automatically prefixed with `/api/v1/plugins/<plugin-id>/`:\n\n```cpp\nvoid PluginManager::registerPluginRoutes(const std::string& pluginId, const RouteCollection& routes)\n{\n    const std::string prefix = \"/api/v1/plugins/\" + pluginId;\n\n    for (const auto& route : routes) {\n        const std::string fullPath = prefix + route.path;\n\n        // Convert string method to http::verb\n        auto verb = methodToVerb(route.method);\n\n        // Register with server, wrapping handler with error boundary\n        _server.router().add(verb, fullPath,\n            [handler = route.handler](const HttpRequest& req, HttpResponse& res) {\n                try {\n                    handler(req, res);\n                } catch (const std::exception& ex) {\n                    res.result(http::status::internal_server_error);\n                    res.body() = R\"({\"error\":\")\" + std::string(ex.what()) + R\"(\"})\";\n                }\n            });\n\n        _pluginRoutes[pluginId].push_back(fullPath);\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 2.3 Server Class Modifications\n\nThe `Server` class (`src/core/Server.h`) needs minor modifications:\n\n1. **Expose Router Reference**: Add a method to access the router for the `PluginManager`:\n\n```cpp\n// In Server class\nRouter& router() noexcept { return router_; }\nconst Router& router() const noexcept { return router_; }\n```\n\n2. **Route Removal Support**: Add capability to remove routes (for hot-reload):\n\n```cpp\n// In Router class (src/core/Server.h:62-98)\nvoid remove(std::string_view pattern)\n{\n    std::unique_lock lock(mutex_);\n    auto it = std::remove_if(routes_.begin(), routes_.end(),\n        [&pattern](const Route& r) {\n            // Compare compiled regex pattern\n            return r.patternStr == pattern;\n        });\n    routes_.erase(it, routes_.end());\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. EventBus Integration Design\n\n### 3.1 Current EventBus API\n\nThe EventBus (`src/core/EventBus.h:180-319`) already provides a robust publish/subscribe API:\n\n```cpp\ntemplate <typename EventT>\n[[nodiscard]] SubscriptionToken subscribe(\n    std::function<void(const EventT&)> callback,\n    EventDelivery delivery = EventDelivery::Async);\n\ntemplate <typename EventT, typename... Args>\nvoid publish(Args&&... args);\n```\n\nKey features:\n- Type-safe subscriptions using template parameters\n- RAII `SubscriptionToken` for automatic unsubscription\n- Sync and Async delivery modes\n- Thread pool for async dispatch\n\n### 3.2 Plugin Access to EventBus\n\n#### 3.2.1 Context Injection Pattern\n\nThe recommended approach is to inject the `EventBus` reference during plugin initialization via a context object:\n\n```cpp\n// In new file: src/interfaces/PluginContext.h\n\nnamespace mbs::plugin {\n\nstruct PluginContext {\n    EventBus&              eventBus;       // Reference to central event bus\n    const nlohmann::json&  configuration;  // Plugin-specific configuration\n    Logger*                logger;         // Optional logger instance\n    std::filesystem::path  dataDirectory;  // Plugin's data storage path\n\n    // Read-only access to system information\n    std::string            pluginId;\n    Version                hostVersion;\n};\n\n} // namespace mbs::plugin\n```\n\n#### 3.2.2 PluginManager Modifications for EventBus Access\n\nThe `PluginManager` already has a reference to the `EventBus` (from `src/core/PluginManager.cpp:140`):\n\n```cpp\nPluginManager::PluginManager(EventBus& bus) : _eventBus(bus)\n```\n\nCurrently, plugins subscribe via `handle->instance->subscribe(_eventBus)` (line 226). This should be enhanced to pass a `PluginContext` during initialization:\n\n```cpp\n// Modified loadPlugin method\nvoid PluginManager::loadPlugin(const std::filesystem::path& libPath)\n{\n    // ... existing library loading code ...\n\n    // Build plugin context\n    PluginContext ctx {\n        .eventBus      = _eventBus,\n        .configuration = loadPluginConfig(pluginId),\n        .logger        = Logger::acquire(pluginId),\n        .dataDirectory = getPluginDataDir(pluginId),\n        .pluginId      = pluginId,\n        .hostVersion   = currentVersion()\n    };\n\n    // Initialize plugin with context (new interface method)\n    pluginPtr->initialize(ctx);\n\n    // ... rest of loading code ...\n}\n```\n\n### 3.3 System Events for Plugins\n\nPlugins should be able to subscribe to standardized system events:\n\n```cpp\n// In new file: src/core/SystemEvents.h\n\nnamespace mbs::events {\n\nstruct UserCreated {\n    std::string userId;\n    std::string email;\n    std::chrono::system_clock::time_point timestamp;\n};\n\nstruct PaymentSuccessful {\n    std::string transactionId;\n    std::string userId;\n    double amount;\n    std::string currency;\n};\n\nstruct DashboardSaved {\n    std::string dashboardId;\n    std::string ownerId;\n};\n\nstruct PluginLoaded {\n    std::string pluginId;\n    Version version;\n};\n\nstruct PluginUnloaded {\n    std::string pluginId;\n};\n\n} // namespace mbs::events\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. New Plugin Interface Design\n\n### 4.1 Interface Design Decision\n\n**Recommendation**: Create a new, more comprehensive `IPlugin` interface rather than modifying `ITile`.\n\n**Justification**:\n1. **Separation of Concerns**: `ITile` is specifically for visual dashboard components; backend integration is a different responsibility\n2. **Backward Compatibility**: Existing tile-only plugins continue to work unchanged\n3. **Single Responsibility**: The new `IPlugin` orchestrates plugin lifecycle and capabilities; `ITile` remains focused on rendering\n4. **Extensibility**: New plugin types (e.g., data providers, authenticators) can implement `IPlugin` without `ITile`\n\n### 4.2 Proposed IPlugin Interface\n\n```cpp\n// In new file: src/interfaces/IPlugin.h\n\n#pragma once\n\n#include <chrono>\n#include <memory>\n#include <string>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include \"PluginContext.h\"\n#include \"IPluginRoute.h\"\n#include \"ITile.h\"\n\nnamespace mbs::plugin {\n\n/**\n * API version for the new plugin interface.\n * Increment on breaking changes.\n */\nconstexpr std::uint32_t PLUGIN_API_VERSION = 0x0200;  // 2.0.0\n\n/**\n * Plugin metadata descriptor.\n */\nstruct PluginDescriptor {\n    std::string id;           // Globally unique identifier (e.g., \"com.acme.analytics\")\n    std::string name;         // Human-readable name\n    std::string version;      // Semantic version string\n    std::string author;       // Author/organization\n    std::string description;  // Brief description\n    std::string license;      // License identifier (e.g., \"MIT\", \"Apache-2.0\")\n\n    // Capability flags\n    bool providesRoutes  = false;\n    bool providesTiles   = false;\n    bool providesEvents  = false;\n};\n\n/**\n * Tile factory registration structure.\n */\nstruct TileFactory {\n    std::string tileTypeId;   // Unique identifier for this tile type\n    std::string displayName;  // Human-readable name\n    std::function<std::unique_ptr<ITile>()> factory;\n};\n\n/**\n * Main plugin interface for the enhanced plugin system.\n *\n * All shared libraries must expose factory functions with signatures:\n *   extern \"C\" IPlugin* mbsCreatePlugin();\n *   extern \"C\" void mbsDestroyPlugin(IPlugin*);\n *   extern \"C\" std::uint32_t mbsPluginApiVersion();\n */\nclass IPlugin {\npublic:\n    virtual ~IPlugin() = default;\n\n    // =========================================================================\n    // Metadata\n    // =========================================================================\n\n    /**\n     * Returns the plugin descriptor containing metadata.\n     * Must be valid immediately after construction.\n     */\n    [[nodiscard]] virtual const PluginDescriptor& descriptor() const noexcept = 0;\n\n    // =========================================================================\n    // Lifecycle\n    // =========================================================================\n\n    /**\n     * Called once after construction to initialize the plugin.\n     * The plugin should:\n     *   - Store a reference to the EventBus from context\n     *   - Subscribe to any events it needs\n     *   - Perform any one-time setup\n     *\n     * May throw to indicate initialization failure.\n     *\n     * @param context Runtime context with service references\n     */\n    virtual void initialize(const PluginContext& context) = 0;\n\n    /**\n     * Called when the plugin should clean up and prepare for unloading.\n     * Must be idempotent and never throw.\n     * All EventBus subscriptions should be released here.\n     */\n    virtual void shutdown() noexcept = 0;\n\n    // =========================================================================\n    // HTTP Route Registration\n    // =========================================================================\n\n    /**\n     * Returns the collection of HTTP routes this plugin wants to register.\n     * Called once after initialize().\n     * Routes will be automatically prefixed with /api/v1/plugins/<plugin-id>/.\n     *\n     * @return Collection of route definitions (may be empty)\n     */\n    [[nodiscard]] virtual RouteCollection getRoutes() const { return {}; }\n\n    // =========================================================================\n    // Tile Factories\n    // =========================================================================\n\n    /**\n     * Returns the tile factories this plugin provides.\n     * Called once after initialize().\n     *\n     * @return Collection of tile factories (may be empty)\n     */\n    [[nodiscard]] virtual std::vector<TileFactory> getTileFactories() const { return {}; }\n\n    // =========================================================================\n    // Event Handling (Optional)\n    // =========================================================================\n\n    /**\n     * Called when a subscribed event is received.\n     * Default implementation does nothing.\n     *\n     * Note: Plugins should prefer using the typed EventBus::subscribe() API\n     * directly for type-safe event handling. This method is provided for\n     * generic event handling scenarios.\n     */\n    virtual void onEvent(const std::string& topic, const nlohmann::json& payload) {}\n};\n\n/**\n * Macro for declaring a plugin implementation with proper C exports.\n */\n#define DECLARE_MOSAIC_PLUGIN(PLUGIN_IMPL)                                      \\\n    extern \"C\" {                                                                \\\n        [[maybe_unused]]                                                        \\\n        ::mbs::plugin::IPlugin* mbsCreatePlugin()                               \\\n        {                                                                       \\\n            return new PLUGIN_IMPL();                                           \\\n        }                                                                       \\\n                                                                                \\\n        [[maybe_unused]]                                                        \\\n        void mbsDestroyPlugin(::mbs::plugin::IPlugin* ptr) noexcept             \\\n        {                                                                       \\\n            delete ptr;                                                         \\\n        }                                                                       \\\n                                                                                \\\n        [[maybe_unused]]                                                        \\\n        std::uint32_t mbsPluginApiVersion() noexcept                            \\\n        {                                                                       \\\n            return ::mbs::plugin::PLUGIN_API_VERSION;                           \\\n        }                                                                       \\\n    }\n\n} // namespace mbs::plugin\n```\n\n### 4.3 Example Plugin Implementation\n\n```cpp\n// Example: Analytics Plugin with HTTP endpoint and EventBus subscription\n\n#include <mbs/plugin/IPlugin.h>\n#include <mbs/core/EventBus.h>\n#include <mbs/events/SystemEvents.h>\n\nclass AnalyticsPlugin final : public mbs::plugin::IPlugin {\npublic:\n    const mbs::plugin::PluginDescriptor& descriptor() const noexcept override {\n        static const mbs::plugin::PluginDescriptor desc {\n            .id          = \"com.example.analytics\",\n            .name        = \"Analytics Plugin\",\n            .version     = \"1.0.0\",\n            .author      = \"Example Corp\",\n            .description = \"Tracks user events and provides analytics API\",\n            .license     = \"MIT\",\n            .providesRoutes = true,\n            .providesTiles  = false,\n            .providesEvents = true\n        };\n        return desc;\n    }\n\n    void initialize(const mbs::plugin::PluginContext& ctx) override {\n        _eventBus = &ctx.eventBus;\n        _pluginId = ctx.pluginId;\n\n        // Subscribe to user creation events\n        _userCreatedToken = _eventBus->subscribe<mbs::events::UserCreated>(\n            [this](const auto& event) {\n                trackUserCreation(event);\n            });\n\n        // Subscribe to payment events\n        _paymentToken = _eventBus->subscribe<mbs::events::PaymentSuccessful>(\n            [this](const auto& event) {\n                trackPayment(event);\n            });\n    }\n\n    void shutdown() noexcept override {\n        // Tokens auto-unsubscribe on destruction, but explicit release is cleaner\n        _userCreatedToken = {};\n        _paymentToken = {};\n    }\n\n    mbs::plugin::RouteCollection getRoutes() const override {\n        return {\n            {\n                .method = \"GET\",\n                .path = \"/stats\",\n                .description = \"Get analytics statistics\",\n                .handler = [this](const auto& req, auto& res) {\n                    handleGetStats(req, res);\n                }\n            },\n            {\n                .method = \"POST\",\n                .path = \"/track\",\n                .description = \"Track custom event\",\n                .handler = [this](const auto& req, auto& res) {\n                    handleTrackEvent(req, res);\n                }\n            }\n        };\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    void trackUserCreation(const mbs::events::UserCreated& event) {\n        // Store analytics data\n    }\n\n    void trackPayment(const mbs::events::PaymentSuccessful& event) {\n        // Store payment analytics\n    }\n\n    void handleGetStats(const HttpRequest& req, HttpResponse& res) {\n        res.body() = R\"({\"totalUsers\": 1234, \"totalRevenue\": 5678.90})\";\n        res.set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\");\n    }\n\n    void handleTrackEvent(const HttpRequest& req, HttpResponse& res) {\n        // Process custom tracking\n        res.result(http::status::accepted);\n    }\n\n    mbs::core::EventBus* _eventBus = nullptr;\n    std::string _pluginId;\n    mbs::core::SubscriptionToken _userCreatedToken;\n    mbs::core::SubscriptionToken _paymentToken;\n};\n\nDECLARE_MOSAIC_PLUGIN(AnalyticsPlugin)\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Modified Plugin Lifecycle in PluginManager\n\n### 5.1 Updated Loading Sequence\n\n```cpp\nvoid PluginManager::loadPlugin(const std::filesystem::path& libPath)\n{\n    // 1. Open library\n    auto handle = std::make_unique<PluginHandle>();\n    handle->dlHandle = openLibrary(libPath);\n\n    // 2. Check API version compatibility\n    auto versionFn = loadSymbol<VersionFn>(handle->dlHandle, \"mbsPluginApiVersion\");\n    if (versionFn && versionFn() != PLUGIN_API_VERSION) {\n        throw MBSException(\"Incompatible plugin API version\");\n    }\n\n    // 3. Resolve factory functions\n    auto createFn = loadSymbol<CreatePluginFn>(handle->dlHandle, \"mbsCreatePlugin\");\n    auto destroyFn = loadSymbol<DestroyPluginFn>(handle->dlHandle, \"mbsDestroyPlugin\");\n\n    // 4. Create plugin instance\n    std::unique_ptr<IPlugin> pluginPtr(createFn(), destroyFn);\n\n    // 5. Build context\n    const auto& desc = pluginPtr->descriptor();\n    PluginContext ctx {\n        .eventBus      = _eventBus,\n        .configuration = loadPluginConfig(desc.id),\n        .logger        = Logger::acquire(desc.id),\n        .dataDirectory = getPluginDataDir(desc.id),\n        .pluginId      = desc.id,\n        .hostVersion   = currentVersion()\n    };\n\n    // 6. Initialize plugin (may subscribe to events)\n    pluginPtr->initialize(ctx);\n\n    // 7. Register HTTP routes (if any)\n    if (desc.providesRoutes) {\n        auto routes = pluginPtr->getRoutes();\n        registerPluginRoutes(desc.id, routes);\n    }\n\n    // 8. Register tile factories (if any)\n    if (desc.providesTiles) {\n        auto factories = pluginPtr->getTileFactories();\n        for (const auto& factory : factories) {\n            _tileRegistry.registerFactory(desc.id, factory);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // 9. Store handle\n    handle->instance = std::move(pluginPtr);\n    handle->metadata = buildMetadata(desc, libPath);\n    handle->loadTimestamp = clock::now();\n\n    // 10. Emit telemetry\n    Telemetry::instance().emit(\"plugin.loaded\", {\n        {\"id\", desc.id},\n        {\"version\", desc.version},\n        {\"providesRoutes\", desc.providesRoutes},\n        {\"providesTiles\", desc.providesTiles}\n    });\n\n    _handles.emplace_back(std::move(handle));\n}\n```\n\n### 5.2 Updated Unloading Sequence\n\n```cpp\nvoid PluginManager::unloadPlugin(const std::string& pluginId)\n{\n    auto it = findPluginById(pluginId);\n    if (it == _handles.end()) {\n        throw MBSException(\"Plugin not found: \" + pluginId);\n    }\n\n    auto& handle = *it;\n    const auto& desc = handle->instance->descriptor();\n\n    // 1. Unregister HTTP routes\n    if (desc.providesRoutes) {\n        unregisterPluginRoutes(pluginId);\n    }\n\n    // 2. Unregister tile factories\n    if (desc.providesTiles) {\n        _tileRegistry.unregisterFactories(pluginId);\n    }\n\n    // 3. Shutdown plugin (releases EventBus subscriptions)\n    handle->instance->shutdown();\n\n    // 4. Destroy instance\n    handle->instance.reset();\n\n    // 5. Close library\n    closeLibrary(handle->dlHandle);\n\n    // 6. Remove from collection\n    _handles.erase(it);\n\n    // 7. Emit telemetry\n    Telemetry::instance().emit(\"plugin.unloaded\", {{\"id\", pluginId}});\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 6. Summary of Required Changes\n\n### 6.1 New Files to Create\n\n| File | Purpose |\n|------|---------|\n| `src/interfaces/IPlugin.h` | New comprehensive plugin interface |\n| `src/interfaces/PluginContext.h` | Context object for plugin initialization |\n| `src/interfaces/IPluginRoute.h` | HTTP route definition structures |\n| `src/core/SystemEvents.h` | Standardized system event types |\n\n### 6.2 Files to Modify\n\n| File | Changes |\n|------|---------|\n| `src/core/PluginManager.h` | Add `Server&` reference, route registration methods |\n| `src/core/PluginManager.cpp` | Update `loadPlugin`/`unloadPlugin` with new lifecycle |\n| `src/core/Server.h` | Expose `router()` accessor, add route removal to `Router` |\n| `src/core/Server.cpp` | Implement route removal functionality |\n\n### 6.3 Backward Compatibility\n\nThe existing `ITile` interface (`src/interfaces/ITile.h`) remains unchanged. Plugins that only provide tiles can continue using the simpler tile-factory registration pattern through the new `IPlugin::getTileFactories()` method.\n\n---\n\n## 7. Security Considerations\n\n1. **Route Namespacing**: All plugin routes are automatically prefixed, preventing namespace collisions and unauthorized access to core routes\n2. **Error Boundaries**: Plugin route handlers are wrapped in try-catch to prevent plugin exceptions from crashing the server\n3. **Capability Flags**: Plugins must declare their capabilities, enabling host-level permission checks\n4. **API Version Checking**: Incompatible plugins are rejected at load time\n\n---\n\n## 8. Conclusion\n\nThis design provides a clean architectural extension to MosaicBoard Studio's plugin system that:\n\n1. **Maintains Decoupling**: Plugins interact with the system through well-defined interfaces; the `PluginManager` mediates between plugins and core components\n2. **Preserves Backward Compatibility**: Existing `ITile`-only plugins continue to work\n3. **Enables Deep Integration**: Plugins can now register HTTP endpoints and subscribe to system events\n4. **Follows Established Patterns**: The design leverages existing patterns in the codebase (factory functions, RAII tokens, context injection)\n\nThe key insight is that `IPlugin` serves as the new \"main entry point\" for plugins, while `ITile` remains focused solely on visual rendering - a clean separation of concerns that scales well as plugin capabilities expand.\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

Here's a summary of my analysis and recommendations:\n\n## Summary\n\nI analyzed the MosaicBoard Studio codebase to understand the existing plugin architecture and designed an extension to enable plugins to register HTTP API endpoints and subscribe to system events via the EventBus.\n\n### Key Findings from Code Analysis:\n\n1. **PluginManager** (`src/core/PluginManager.h/cpp`): Uses `dlopen`/`dlsym` for dynamic plugin loading with `mbsCreatePlugin`/`mbsDestroyPlugin` factory symbols.
